Third slice: NMRNet++ through the normal NMR API provider workflow
Date: 2026-10-18
Author: repository coordinator
Status: 003 complete within the locally qualified scope below. R53 delivered both SDF ZIP records/eight C shifts through one actual API Job; R54 observed all four real-interpreter ambiguity decisions and all three MOL/PDB/MOL2 choices. Evidence and scientific-guide/API handoff are retained. Browser submission is not a qualification gate under the user's correction. Scientific, deployment and operating limits below remain explicit.

Completion audit, 2026-10-23 (API source154be58; interpreter sourcee211390; R54)
================================================================
This active list supersedes older open-ended status wording, while preserving
original outcomes, failed exits and scope. It grants no new external effects.

Required product outcomes
--------------------------
1. SATISFIED: the authorized two-record SDF ZIP API outcome. The user explicitly
   corrected "GUI is not SUT here"; no browser-submission prerequisite remains.
   R53's maintained provider/qualify/api/real-sdf-zip uses the signed API client
   and reuses R46's UXc3mVpW native result/R48's member request-hash joins. Its
   first actual run reserved one Upload, then received502 on the original PUT.
   The user then authorized a service-only restart and original-Upload recovery.
   The live storage owner confirmed absent bytes before renewed capability/PUT;
   the same Upload finalized and one API Job succeeded. All eight carbon shifts,
   ordered source records and scientific facts equal R46 exactly; all original
   native/host work retired and terminal evidence is archived. No duplicate
   reservation/Job or paid rerun occurred. Full IDs and hashes appear in R53.
   Existing R24/R25/R28/R32 API paths and R44 GUI readback retain their own scope.
2. SATISFIED: real semantic decisions for ambiguous/unsupported input. R24/R25/R28 already
   show the selected real endpoint on three valid API routes; R32 covers N>1;
   R24 includes genuine correction. R45/R49's interpreter_ambiguity.json four-case
   batch now passed4/4 against the real selected endpoint: absent units/count/
   record and unsupported input, with zero preparation attempts/corrections.
   R54 records the newly authorized run; no Jobs/science were supplied.
   R53 adds real multi-geometry
   Upload selection. Passing proves these decisions, not universal understanding.
   Real MOL/PDB/MOL2 argument choice now passed3/3 inR54, using the authored
   normative/atom_order_v2000.mol, models.pdb and mixed_records.mol2 metadata,
   requested units/order and existing reader-option admission. No parser or
   scientific effects were invoked. Compose these observations with the existing
   actual reader/science/acquisition proofs; no new full matrix is required.
3. SATISFIED: actual outcomes and compact source/runtime/scientific receipts are
   retained inR53/R54; current notes and scientific guide reflect their scope.
   docs/index.html is the scientific entry; docs/provider.md owns operation.
   stage_api_config.py already wires the guide into the description/Hello path;
   R53's actual API provider directory response contains that guide URL in
   provider_description; R54 retains its exact bytes/hash without another Job.
   The observed stale native-history warning was fixed at its owner in087a090.
   These outcomes close003's current scope, not remote deployment or universal
   semantic/accuracy claims. Original failures and historical proposals remain.

Existing owner/composition proof sufficient within recorded limits
------------------------------------------------------------------
- Science: R13b/lNCAQMBR covers137 outputs/64 independent positions at zero
  tolerance; R15b/A2nFXFz6 covers real128-atom H/C capacity and per-role resources.
  R24/R25/R28/R32 cover actual API paths and supplied/generated N1/N3 identity.
  No affected scientific code/oracle changed in R50: reuse these observations.
- Formats: R37/provider-native-formats.QFIJkrxt retains nine native cases,
  15 geometries/54 finite H/C rows and contained receipt review. Both original
  host exits stay failed. R46 adds the carbon-only SDF; R47/R48 qualify ZIP/member
  handling and exact request correspondence. Deterministic readers own atoms,
  units and record order. No paid per-format matrix is required merely to repeat
  those checks; R54 separately observes real MOL/PDB/MOL2 argument choice.
- Repeated use: R36/R40 connect three Jobs, lost terminal ACK and production
  stopped recovery with recording science. R39 separately covers installed
  inspection/replay/idle restart. Native R13b/R15b/R37/R46 cover exact replay
  without new science. This owner composition suffices; it is not a deployed
  multi-Job/native-crash experiment, and does not require buying that combination.
- Failures/recipients: R41/R42 cover input deadlines and phase/current-condition
  recovery; R43 covers bounded prediction replacement and synthetic cgroup OOM.
  Note018 records receiving error/cancellation/cleanup and permission proofs;
  note019 supersedes its old file sink with qualified bounded Docker logs.
  Configured service failure receipts cover requester guidance. Add one focused
  proof for an actual missing causal link, not every fault/route/model combination.

Limits and separate open operating work

GUI fixtures request17 for exact comparison; no extra paid default/other-digit
gate is required solely because an old note says live precision is unqualified.
R24 already supplies real correction; do not manufacture another repair.
Generated-geometry accuracy, five-fold ensemble/GPU results and actual released-
model OOM retry equivalence are not established by the narrower evidence above.

R52 supersedes note017/9b89802's design-only storage status. Native sequence/
release and required finite confirmed history now have focused owner/service and
installed-runtime proof. They bound healthy operation; no physical disk quota,
power-loss guarantee or indefinite failure-free service is claimed. Existing
excess/malformed history and uncertain cleanup still require explicit inspection,
not automatic migration or deletion. Scientific capsules remain intact. Use the
refreshed controller and freshly staged required retention configuration for the
next reviewed API run; do not reuse R48's old image/configuration as current.
Remote/HA deployment, ensemble/training/GPU work and CI remain outside this slice.

R53: API SDF ZIP submission, authorized scope (2026-10-23)
---------------------------------------------------------
The user's correction, "GUI is not SUT here. Let the worker create the job via
API", replaces the browser-submission requirement above for this provider
qualification. Existing GUI readback remains a separate claim; three new browser
submissions are not a closure gate. Other selected-model ambiguity/format batches
remain separately unapproved.

Maintained target provider/qualify/api/real-sdf-zip reuses api_prediction.py's
installed NMR API signing/canonicalization/Upload owners and the normal provider
launcher. It creates one Upload, publishes its bytes once, finalizes it, creates
one closed Job, attaches the Upload, selects the provider and opens that Job.
Uncertain effects retain original identities and never trigger resubmission.
The existing gui_sdf.json instruction is shared; its filename does not imply a
browser produced this API request. Only the necessary API source files are
captured from committed Git blobs. Readback/process/host budgets are900/1200/1220s.

Source: normative/butane_conformers.sdf,1010 bytes, SHA256
  c8007f5a1a80ad080bb972caf005bbb4fedcd5a9bd15b892e1b640d2f01e4374
The client produces one deterministic ZIP_STORED member with a fixed DOS epoch;
it pins that original SDF and the R46 result before any API request. The actual
published ZIP hash/length comes from the signed finalized Upload and is compared
separately from the member hash/length. No existing reference is regenerated.
R46 scientific-result.json SHA256:
  bd5adb6f47ac7b5b739ae4c52ce36d308195bae56b6233c0d07e22918227a3a3
Exact retained path: .work/qualification/provider-service-nmrnet-native-service-
UXc3mVpW/formats/sdf-carbon-only/scientific-result.json (one joined path).
R48 and gui_science.mjs::compareSubmittedScience supply the reviewed source/
archive and ordered scientific comparison contract. Both records preserve four
supplied C atoms, no generated atoms/coordinates and eight C predictions total;
selected models, native runtime/image, request hashes, binary32 coordinates,
preparation, coverage and predictions must equal the retained reference exactly.
This proves repeatability/composition, not independent chemical-shift accuracy.

The authorized model is openai-gpt-5-6-luna at api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions,
model gpt-5.6-luna, reasoning_effort none, max6turns/Attempt. The API may schedule
a replacement Attempt; the client must not create another Job. No other model
batch, browser run, deployment or scientific matrix is authorized here.

Focused offline qualification: signed client34/0.697s, staging/composition15/
3.650s, Ruff0.15.15, bash syntax and diff whitespace pass. Refusal witnesses
cover altered source before API dispatch, missing/reordered/changed records,
wrong member/outer archive/Upload attribution, one PUT and one opened Job, and
the exact time profile and source mounts. The synthetic fixture does not claim
accuracy. Independent scientific and launcher review found no further mismatch
after removing the unrelated GUI input dependency. Actual run evidence follows
only after observation; this section alone establishes no new API/model result.

Active roadmap: useful, reproducible NMR predictions, 2026-10-23
================================================================
The repository has two connected aims: reproduce NMRNet++ H/C chemical-shift
prediction faithfully, and make it usable through the normal NMR API workflow.
Success means a commissioner submits molecular input and an analysis receiver
obtains understandable per-atom predictions with enough provenance to assess and
reproduce them. Containment, provider-core, launchers and checks support that
transaction. Their individual completion is not the product milestone.

This roadmap supersedes the earlier sequence in the 2026-10-20 chat and the
initial "Implementation slices and acceptance" section at c0bb2eb. It changes
sequencing, not the required scientific, containment, error or recovery contracts.
The dated implementation entries below retain their original evidence and limits.
The user resumed implementation after this roadmap revision on 2026-10-21.
The earlier pause remains a historical checkpoint; R24/R25/R28/R32 now update the
starting point. The current gates above adjudicate which remaining outcomes need
new observations and which are sufficiently covered by existing owner evidence.

Starting point
--------------
Native local witnesses already cover supplied coordinates, verified Uploads and
SMILES conformer generation; the four-reference population and real 128-atom
capacity have separate retained evidence (R13b/R15b below). The foreground caller
and installed startup helper were committed at c0bb2eb with focused checks. The
controller refresh and first actual API transaction through the complete foreground
caller were qualified with scripted interpretation (R21). The selected OpenAI
endpoint has since delivered all three input routes through the actual API:
pasted coordinates with one correction/two turns (R24), generated CCl/N1 in one
uncorrected turn (R25), and attached XYZ after inspection in two uncorrected turns
(R28). R32 adds the representative CCCC/N3 case in one uncorrected turn, with
three separately attributed geometries and successful normal shutdown. Pasted/
Upload native inputs and predictions match the pinned numerical oracle; generated
N1/N3 establish observed provenance/coverage without an
independent numerical reference. R28's overall target failed on cancellation of
a local liveness observer, despite confirmed exact retirement of all containers.
R36 adds configured successive Jobs and exact stopped-store recovery before a
third Job; R37 has native results for nine supplied-format cases, with both host
target failures retained. R39 qualifies installed operator inspection, exact
terminal replay and ordinary idle restart against synthetic local TLS, without
new science. R40 joins service-produced outcomes to the production stopped
inspection/recovery functions and subsequent Job, using recording science.
R44 now qualifies existing-result readback/download through the actual local GUI
for pasted coordinates, generated N1/N3 and XYZ Upload. It creates no new Jobs and
does not qualify GUI submission, additional real-model multi-geometry Upload/
format/ambiguity coverage, the broader connected fault matrix or final handoff.
Do not rebuild scientific machinery or present completed foundations as new work.

1. Deliver one prediction through the actual API.
   Qualified locally in R21 and with the real endpoint in R24/R25/R28/R32.
   The requirements below remain the repeatable transaction contract, not a claim
   that 003 is closed.
   Use one existing supplied-geometry fixture and the released prediction models.
   The path is: discover/select the offering, submit an authenticated Job, read
   the supplied geometry, predict H/C shifts, publish, and read back the result
   through the actual API. Use the installed foreground service entrypoint.
   Compare delivered atom indices and shifts with the applicable existing fixture
   evidence. Retain the exact executed geometries, selected model/scaler identities
   and runtime facts with the result. Scientific correspondence is part of this
   first transaction and remains required in every later milestone.

   Refresh the controller image for launch_capture and run its focused installed
   checks. Fix the startup, routing, permission, shutdown or diagnostic defects
   that prevent this transaction or violate its existing safety contracts. Verify
   actual grants while exercising that same run. Reuse the API's existing contained
   environment and identity workflow; this authorizes no sibling source changes.
   A short scripted-model/local-peer smoke run may establish the connections first;
   identify it as such. It does not close actual API or real interpretation proof.

   Identify API access, signing authority, endpoint/model, trust, explicit network
   interface and GUI prerequisites upfront. Missing external access is a named
   blocker to that qualification, while useful contained work can continue. Never
   invent credentials, endpoints, paid-call authority or deployment success.
   Exit: one submitted Job yields a scientifically traceable result through the
   actual API, with confirmed native cleanup and preserved input/result evidence.
   Record whether interpretation was scripted or real and whether GUI readback
   was exercised; remaining parts stay explicit for milestones 2 and 4.

2. Make the three agreed requests work with the real interpreter.
   Qualify the selected real endpoint early, as soon as its explicit prerequisites
   are available. The required requests are SMILES + conformer count N, pasted
   coordinates for one conformer, and an Upload with instructions. Include SMILES
   N > 1 and a multi-geometry Upload. Carry each request through prediction and
   receiver readback, using the existing API and scientific owners.

   Reuse the existing XYZ/MOL/SDF/PDB/MOL2 fixtures to finish declared file-format
   coverage. Preserve supplied atoms/order, selected records and explicit units;
   distinguish estimated RDKit geometries from supplied ones and record generated
   H/parent correspondence. Do not check uploaded records for molecular equivalence.
   Exercise real ambiguity and correction: unclear units/count/record selection,
   unsupported input and model-reported problems. Check that interpretation follows
   the request; deterministic validation alone cannot establish that fact. Model-
   supplied pasted/SMILES text must remain honestly attributed, not described as
   byte-exact extraction from the original Job. Never silently repair chemistry.
   Exit: a commissioner can use every advertised route and format, or receive
   useful guidance, and the receiver can identify each geometry and predicted atom.
   A model-authored result explanation remains attributed and qualified as such.

3. Make repeated use and interrupted work reliable.
   Exercise the same service over successive Jobs, graceful stop and restart.
   Cover capacity exhaustion, cancellation, interpreter/source outages, failure
   after partial H/C computation, lost API acknowledgements and storage/cleanup
   faults at the owners actually used by this workflow. Reuse existing fault
   witnesses; add only missing connected behavior. Publish all-or-nothing results.
   Prove exact retained-outcome replay without repeating successful interpretation,
   geometry generation or prediction. Confirm unresolved ownership blocks unsafe
   replacement and that forced stop cannot appear as a clean completed analysis.

   Complete the operator's stopped inspection/archive/restart procedure, keeping
   configuration, durable Attempt state and useful evidence. Verify the actual
   caller's private creation, mounted permissions and explicit sole network routes.
   Safe errors must reach commissioner, receiver and operator through the channels
   they use, including protected retained diagnostics outside a terminal; secrets
   must not propagate. Preserve operation, delivery uncertainty and cleanup causes.
   Exit: another Job and a restart are supported without orphaned work, duplicate
   science, hidden errors or unexplained operator intervention.

4. Hand over a reproducible offering and close 003.
   R44 qualifies the existing-result receiving surface for all three routes,
   including generated N3; actual GUI submission and remaining requirements below
   stay open. The running dev GUI reports no source commit; this is an observed
   receiving witness, not attestation of a deployed GUI source revision.
   Finish actual GUI submission/readback for all three routes, using the final
   admitted service and real interpreter. Confirm that recipients see atom and
   geometry correspondence, ppm units, warnings, provenance and relevant limits.
   Put copyable requests and the tested install/start/stop/recovery steps in the
   README. Lead with what the service does, what users supply and what they receive;
   implementation history stays in notes. Offering text must match delivered
   behavior and the existing API's bounds, with a usable guide link.

   Consolidate the scientific evidence from the delivered paths: exact input and
   output populations, producer transformations, selected artifacts and observed
   runtime. Reuse the four-reference/128-atom evidence where its scientific code,
   inputs, artifacts, runtime and enforced limits remain applicable. Repeat only
   affected qualifications; never regenerate expected values or tune tolerances.
   The qualified scientific configuration uses CPU float32 selected fold-0 H/C
   checkpoints. The offering must not claim the paper's five-fold ensemble accuracy.
   Return separate predictions per geometry; do not invent conformer averaging,
   confidence or experimental data.
   Preserve rights-appropriate inputs, methods, raw observations and precise
   source/runtime/model/prompt/config identities; publish no secrets or raw chats.
   Exit: a fresh operator can reproduce the supported workflow, and every 003
   obligation is either evidenced or explicitly open. An open required gate means
   003 remains open; local TLS alone never establishes deployed API/GUI completion.

How to execute this roadmap
---------------------------
R29 qualifies the foreground observation-race correction offline; R32 passes
normal shutdown without a live race injection. R28's original target exit2 remains.
R35 implements per-Job interpreter-selected output precision offline. Reuse that
proof; the next GUI fixtures select17 without requiring another paid default/
other-digit case. R36/R37/R39/R40 and R41-R43 supply the bounded repeated-use,
native-format, local operator and reviewed failure-owner evidence. Required real
semantic/GUI outcomes and optional proof limits are distinguished above. The
selected real-model ambiguity batch awaits explicit approval.
Treat each supporting fix as part of those outcomes and qualify it at its owner.
Do not put an exhaustive new platform-hardening campaign ahead of them; required admission,
image, credential, explicit-interface, isolation and exact-cleanup gates still apply.
Reuse the independent failure/recovery cases in milestone3; add a focused proof
only for a concrete uncovered causal gap. Arrange real API/model/GUI prerequisites alongside local work so
their absence becomes visible early rather than at final handoff.

Use tests-first coherent slices, focused contained checks and the recorded U/A
and ordinary code reviews. Batch validation of one coherent boundary; recheck it
when changes or findings justify it. Record evidence at each milestone, then
commit reviewed explicit paths. No host Python/RDKit, upstream/sibling edits,
optional package extraction, new generic framework, training, ensemble/GPU work
or standalone public prediction CLI. CI remains deferred. Preserve reproducibility
and rationale with compact reviewed evidence; raw scratch stays private/ignored
and is retained only while its purpose requires it.

Direct input tools and terminal selection: superseding correction, 2026-10-20
==========================================================================
User direction replaces the former byte-span and prepared-reference conversation:
prepare_pasted(source_text, source_format=table|xyz, source_unit),
prepare_smiles(smiles, requested_count), and prepare_upload with the existing
verified Upload reference/options. Pasted and SMILES text becomes bounded,
immutable UTF-8 bytes before worker allocation; existing deterministic readers
and the native generator own parsing, units, atoms and conformer admission.
No offsets, line selectors, substring verification or opaque token echo remain.
Successful preparation terminally selects input in that model turn. Expected
input rejection permits correction in the SAME bounded conversation; operational,
grant, digest, budget and cleanup failures retain their operational owners.

Job ref/fingerprint binds received argument bytes and their digest/provenance to
the Attempt. This is interpreter-supplied text, not verified exact extraction from
the Job or proof of faithful transcription. Reader positions refer to received
source_text. Uploads retain their distinct authenticated original-byte identity.

conversion oracles and internal PreparedAnalysis identity remain exact. Source
and interpreter retirement still precede candidate handoff; durable execution
entry still precedes shift inference. There is no extra submit call or stale
model-reference registry. Normal pasted/SMILES requires one model exchange;
Upload inspection plus preparation requires two. Corrections add turns; no
successful preparation is repeated to confirm an opaque reference.

Correctable Upload input refusals, 2026-10-20
--------------------------------------------
JobUploads raises UploadInputRejected only before any grant for missing inspection,
an unknown inspected member, changed inspected membership, or a selected file
above the fixed per_source_bytes ceiling. The last is known unsupported input,
not provider malfunction. The interpreter converts this owner-typed refusal to
safe feedback in the same conversation, requiring explicit reinspection after
snapshot invalidation. No UploadFailed-wide catch or exception text classifier.
Grant issuance, byte/digest verification, exhausted remaining cumulative bytes,
requests/deadline, and unconfirmed resource cleanup stay operational. Existing
accounting, deadline and possible-grant evidence are preserved; no retry resets it.

D3 offline startup capture, 2026-10-20
-------------------------------------
The new nmrnet_provider.startup_capture helper invokes the existing config.py
admission owner with an explicit internal reader port. It retains exactly each
read_startup_file result after its descriptor has been read and closed; it never
validates one pathname read and then copies a different read. Only consumed
TOML/credential/key/explicit-CA files enter a fresh0700 capture with0600 files.
An unrelated private file is excluded. Repeated paths repeat the original
secret/bounds/ownership admission and must return identical bytes. A host-supplied
aggregate byte ceiling bounds distinct captured bytes; no limit is inferred from
network or lifecycle policy. No HTTP, worker or Attempt storage is acquired.

The original TOML is unchanged. The future caller mounts capture/inputs at the
same original absolute source root, then starts the same immutable controller
image against those frozen bytes. Source_sha256 therefore retains its actual
meaning. System TLS roots remain owned by that image; CLI execution/model
declarations are separate frozen/admitted grants. The helper does not claim to
capture either through this config reader. The source root must stay immutable
during capture; path checks reject parent components/intermediate links rather
than claiming final-component O_NOFOLLOW alone confines a tree.

plan.json is a closed, protected projection of actual host consumers: three
authority sets, native time/embedding/seed/call budgets, state path, shutdown
budgets and original configuration identity. No config object, key or signing
credential representation is serialized. It is published only after whole typed
configuration validation and successful private writes/closes. Partial captures
remain private evidence. Source/capture/writable-store roots must be canonical
and disjoint in both directions; no ready plan is published on refusal.

Authority admission reuses tools.provider_proxy.authority_parts, not a new URL
policy. Port443 is selected only for an omitted port. Explicit:0 and unsupported
IPv6 literals refuse before plan publication. Omitted and custom ports retain
their destinations; API explicit:443 follows its signing owner's normalization.
Model/store explicit:443 retains their existing canonical-URL refusal rather than
rewriting input. The image's existing support-file capture/install closure now
includes provider_proxy.py and bound_http_proxy.py so startup and later relay
entrypoints use the same admission owner. No dependencies or proxy rules changed.

Configured runtime assembly: implementation plan, 2026-10-20
==========================================================
Purpose: make the existing input interpreter, acquisition, native-worker session
and core Attempt lifecycle usable through one explicit production assembly.
Implement and test this assembly before a continuous service loop or deployment
controller. The accepted full-dependency package remains unchanged. This plan
does not authorize sibling/upstream edits, external model calls or deployment.

Current evidence: at 4621bd851700c2d0b3ef662781b07651488e1000, native
scientific-smiles uses actual generation, released prediction, signed local API,
durable entry, exact host retirement and lost-completion replay, but scripts the
chooser and assembles owners inside its test. Upload preparation has real source
acquisition/reader proof with recording prediction. No normal configuration
decoder, credential-artifact parser or production assembly exists. ProviderAPI
has no Hello operation; host tooling prestarts qualification allocations rather
than providing a production allocator. Do not promote these fixtures into a
scheduler, infer external authority, or call assembly proof a working service.

Responsibilities and construction order
---------------------------------------
1. provider_core.credential admits bytes of the API-issued
   nmr.provider.private_signing_credential.v1 document. Preserve its seven fields,
   profile vocabulary, Ed25519 public/private pairing and canonical encodings.
   Reuse existing reference validators. Return a small immutable credential with
   private key excluded from repr. No key generation, registration, file discovery
   or alternative seed/environment credential format belongs in this parser.
2. nmrnet_provider.config decodes one bounded closed TOML document. Group settings
   by existing owners: API/TLS; ordered interpreter endpoints; Upload store and
   budgets; preparation/generation budgets; lifecycle and stable recovery scope.
   Delegate values to SigningIdentity/ChatEndpointSpec, UploadLimits,
   GenerationSettings, StoreLimits and existing lifecycle admission. Do not copy
   their numeric or scientific validators. Accept explicit secret/CA inputs;
   use verified system roots or an explicit CA, never disable TLS verification.
   No ambient credentials/proxies, URL/model defaults, route selector, command
   imports, hot reload or configuration framework. Reject unknown keys without
   echoing their potentially private names/values. Startup file reads are bounded
   regular-file reads, with explicit ownership/mode/symlink rules for secrets.
3. nmrnet_provider.runtime assembles existing owners for one selected authenticated
   Job. Worker ports/factories and observed execution/model/runtime facts come
   from trusted host composition, not from Job text or interpreter arguments.
   Keep lifecycle API transport separate from source API/store and model I/O:
   JobUploads -> InputPreparation(InputInterpreter, allocators) -> NMRNetSession
   -> AttemptController. The interpreter chooses the route. No new start/execute/
   publish state machine, reference registry, transport or replay journal.
4. The outer caller owns retained storage and lifecycle API I/O. Inspect storage
   before requesting fresh allocation or dispatch; retained nonterminal
   obligations require explicit recovery or refusal, not automatic replay.
   A caller supplying preallocated ports retains their setup/refusal cleanup.
   The native fixtures prestart workers before storage admission and therefore
   cannot establish allocation-after-inventory ordering. Input facts must come
   from the outer caller's actual signed ProviderAPI.jobs read and explicit
   selection, not a TOML FeedInputFacts lookalike. Bound any pagination. Keep
   recovery generation stable across restarts;
   disposable native-allocation IDs are not persistent deployment generations.
   Construct JobUploads shortly before use because its total deadline starts
   at construction. Before controller adoption, construction failure leaves
   created resources explicitly caller-owned or retires them with all causes.
   After adoption, settle the controller before releasing its dependencies.
   Unconfirmed retirement retains ownership and blocks replacement; do not race
   an independent session.retire against controller settlement or close retained
   storage while its selected owner still needs it.

Tests-first slices and acceptance
--------------------------------
R1 credential admission: package normative/adversarial tests first. Prove signing
with the admitted key, original API field/profile/reference meanings, exact key
pairing and rejection of substitutions, ambiguous JSON/base64 and unsupported
algorithms. Check safe error text/repr and installed portable guard/inventory.
No dependency acquisition or version change is required.

R2 configuration: focused contained decoder/file tests before implementation.
One valid concrete configuration produces actual owned policy objects. Unknown
fields, duplicate/malformed TOML, missing endpoints/secrets, identity mismatch,
invalid trust and inconsistent native deadline settings fail before any worker,
network or journal acquisition. Public observations exclude secrets and private
paths; retain useful owned field/operation context. No placeholder deployment
identity or guessed endpoint makes a configuration usable.

R3 assembly: focused local TLS integration through the production factory, with
recording native ports first. Prove all three routes, source/API credential
separation, construction/refusal cleanup, no scientific dispatch after input or
cleanup failure, cancellation, and exact durable/publication behavior. Reuse the
existing store/controller and structured normative/adversarial test layout.
Include actual signed jobs-page discovery -> selected input facts -> verified
Job-input retrieval. Wrong input identity fails before preparation. A retained
obligation refuses with zero allocator calls/dispatch, unchanged records, and
confirmed cleanup of any caller-owned preallocated ports; refusal does not recover.
Preserve the existing explicitly direct binding separately: NMRNetSession with
input_preparation=None still consumes exact coordinate-JSON bytes through
prepare_direct_job; malformed declared JSON remains terminal without a model
call. Keep its focused tests and native direct proof. The new interpreted assembly
supports the three agreed interpreted routes; it does not claim automatic direct-
JSON detection. Do not guess a route from syntax, add a deployment route selector,
or fall through after JSON failure. Any future unified direct-input declaration
needs an explicit source contract; retaining the direct binding is not that claim.
Expose a named hierarchical focused target; no temporary review script or
standalone diagnostic command is the product.

R4 native binding: update the existing scientific-smiles proof to call the same
production assembly and configured InputInterpreter against a separate local
TLS model peer. Keep qualified CCCC/N=3, seed/settings and independent H-parent
oracle in smiles.json; rigid CCl/N=2 may legitimately shortfall. Preserve its
durable prepared identity, exact generated/native coordinates, host-confirmed
generator retirement before science, full result and lost-completion replay.
Reopen/replay exact terminal bytes without another conversation or science run.

R5 supplied routes: extend that same native lifecycle spine, not another framework.
Pasted: authored nonzero chloromethane table in nm, UTF-8 prefix, exact selected
byte span, independent x10 angstrom oracle and original atom positions.
Upload: mixed_records.xyz, unlike records 2,1, exact source bytes/hash/options,
water then chloromethane, original source positions and complete predictions.
Water loads H only; never impose the generated fixture's H/C expectation on it.
Extend host grants, prestarted-reader failure cleanup, source manifests and private
receipts for each scenario. Retain network none, separate preparation/science
grants, current measured resource/deadline bounds and exact-container retirement.
An outer native-fixture guard owns every prestarted-but-unused generator/reader
and prediction grant across setup/choice/refusal failures. InputPreparation owns
only allocations returned by its factories. Preserve the existing extra cleanup
currently supplied by PrestartedGeneratorInput without moving that test subclass
into production or claiming that a closed socket confirms host retirement.
Do not widen a limit or tolerance to make a fixture pass.

For every slice: reproduce missing behavior with focused contained tests, implement,
review code and important U/A, fix concrete findings, repeat to two settled
no-signal rounds, commit explicit -- FILE paths and record actual results here.
Runtime failures remain distinct from correctable input rejection; retain primary
and independent cleanup evidence privately, with safe recipient-specific context.
Do not log raw credentials, model transcripts, source contents or response bodies.

Closure and limits
------------------
This assembly milestone requires R1-R5, actual production-factory witnesses for all
three native routes, passing affected focused conventions and accurate operator
documentation. It does not close 003. Continuous Hello/discovery/readiness,
production host allocation/installation, capacity/population gates, configured
external-model behavior and actual API/GUI delivery remain explicit work. User
credentials, disclosed request material and network interface must be selected
before external invocation; local ephemeral TLS peers require none of them.

R1 implemented: API-issued credential admission, 2026-10-20
----------------------------------------------------------
provider_core.credential ports SECS's existing reader into the installed package,
using the core bounded JSON decoder and shared provider/credential reference
validators. It preserves the original seven-field canonical artifact, profile
vocabulary, Ed25519 pair and key encodings. Safe admission errors retain private
decode causes; private keys are excluded from repr. File permissions and intended
provider/profile binding remain startup responsibilities. No alternate credential
format, issuance, registration or dependency change was introduced.

The new focused make test/unit/provider-core/credential initially failed because
the parser did not exist; the port then passed 14 artifact/signing tests. These
include mismatched/actual non-Ed25519 keys, base64 padding bits, ambiguous JSON,
artifact identity and real signing with the admitted key. Five installed contracts,
seven launcher tests and 37 focused repository conventions passed, all contained
and offline. Changed Python lint/format, shell syntax and whitespace passed.
Three independent source/U/A reviewers returned two settled no-signal rounds.
The source and adaptation rationale are recorded beside the parser and its package
README. This closes R1 only; R2-R5 and normal service remain open.

R2 implemented: explicit startup configuration, 2026-10-20
---------------------------------------------------------
nmrnet_provider.config ports the SECS closed-TOML/descriptor startup pattern,
constructing existing policy objects as NMRPeak does. One public loader reads the
original issued signing credential, binds its provider/profile, admits ordered
interpreter endpoints and returns settings grouped by the actual runtime owners.
It acquires no HTTP, native worker or Attempt journal. Runtime assembly and a
continuous service remain separate work. Source revisions/locations, intentional
port differences and exact configuration fields are recorded in source/README.

One draft setting was removed during sibling review: analysis_kind_ref is now
code-owned peaks_from_mol, following G01's forward-prediction offering and SECS's
ownership of scientific identity. Deployers cannot relabel the computation.
Generation/recovery identities, origins, files and budgets remain explicit.
Extracted existing owner preconditions admit limits before allocating transports,
carriers or a storage executor; the constructors use the same unchanged rules.

The file port completes short reads and checks consumed length, regular-file
status, size, ownership/mode and final-component symlinks on the same descriptor.
Parent mounts remain host-controlled; this does not establish whole-tree safety
or immutable inode contents. Public errors keep owned field/operation reasons;
raw paths/parser evidence and independent close failures remain private. Exact
interruption identity and any previous cause survive a secondary close failure.
Explicit CA certificates are materialized from consumed bytes; empty CA input
cannot silently select system roots. Ambient OpenSSL root overrides and Python
TLS key logging are rejected. The observed checker runtime is CPython 3.12.12;
Lib/ssl.py::create_default_context was inspected directly for this rationale.
source_sha256 identifies only TOML bytes, not credential/CA/runtime closure.

Tests first: the new make test/integration/provider-core/config failed exactly
because the module was absent, then passed 14 focused tests. Actual local TLS
uses the admitted CA after its pathname is replaced; no external service or
native chemistry was contacted. Tests also exercise missing/unsafe secret files,
0400 permissions, owner mismatch, FIFO refusal, short reads/growth, read+close
failure, interruption causes, malformed/closed configuration, identity mismatch,
oversized numbers and forbidden ambient TLS settings. Existing focused input
interpreter tests (17), launcher regressions (7) and provider-core repository
conventions (37) passed. These establish configuration admission only; R3-R5
and normal provider service remain open.
Changed Python lint/format, shell syntax and whitespace checks passed. Three
independent code/authority/provenance reviewers reached two settled no-signal
rounds after the corrections above; the wrong-owner test now targets the key's
actual descriptor rather than accidentally rejecting the configuration file.

R3 implemented: configured Attempt assembly, 2026-10-20
-------------------------------------------------------

existing JobUploads, InputInterpreter, InputPreparation, NMRNetSession and

that source and lifecycle API calls use the same configured identity, rather
than comparing copied credential claims. All HTTP roles retain separate owners.

Assembly checks scope/offering and retained inventory before passive wiring.
Its inventory check does not reserve work; the controller still performs its
own admission/claim checks at run. The caller must run the same pending start
and retains opened store/API lifetime. Scientific preallocations belong to the
caller on refusal, and transfer only when the controller is returned. A returned
but never-run controller can retire its allocation through settle. Cancellation
of preflight may leave the original threaded inventory read pending: the caller
must settle that exact read before retry or close. Lazy input factories remain
owned by the existing preparation owner; partial allocation stays factory-owned.

R5 implemented: supplied native routes on the same lifecycle, 2026-10-20
------------------------------------------------------------------------
tests/integration/provider_core/native_lifecycle.py extracts the qualified R4
spine at 6f8a763: configured assembly, actual interpreter, signed local discovery,
durable execution entry, exact host retirement, complete result and lost-completion
replay. Molecular selection and independent expectations remain route-local.
The host's existing optional source slot is now named geometry and carries either
the generator or reader. No extra allocator, lifecycle, native parser, public route
selector, framework or credential owner was introduced. Three containers remain:
geometry and controller at 512 MiB/2 CPUs/64 PIDs, science at 4 GiB/2 CPUs/64 PIDs;
all read-only, nonroot, network none, cap-drop ALL/no-new-privileges, without swap
beyond memory limits. Preparation receives neither models nor API/model secrets.

make test/integration/provider-scientific-worker/pasted selects an authored
chloromethane table in nm from an exact UTF-8 Job byte span. The independent
expected.json oracle fixes atoms H/Cl/C/H/H and angstrom literals. An initial
places=12 assertion was replaced following monitoring review. For exact source
decimal x, parsed binary64 p=RN(x) and multiplication y=RN(10p), the explicit bound is
  |y - 10x| <= 10|p - x| + 0.5 ULP(RN(10p)).
nm_conversion_oracle derives this allowance using exact Fraction arithmetic from
fixed source/target literals before reader execution; it first proves target=10x.
The test requires radix 2, 53-bit significand and round-to-nearest. For these
fixed finite normal values, parsing contributes its exactly computed error and
one multiplication contributes at most half an ULP; 10 itself is exact. Zero
has zero allowance. No observed output chooses the bound, and no chemical or
decimal tolerance is fitted. Source/hash/span/record/replay identities stay exact.
The scientific unit reference remains input_geometry.coordinate_conversion and
fixture README reference 6. This refinement strengthens fixture proof; it is not
evidence of incorrect runtime chemistry.

make test/integration/provider-scientific-worker/uploads selects XYZ records 2,1:
water then chloromethane, positions [10,11,12] then [3,4,5,6,7]. Actual signed
membership/read-capability operations and a separate TLS store provide verified
source bytes; API/model/store credential separation is checked at each peer.
Reader options, original records, atom order and angstrom coordinates are exact.
Water requires H only; chloromethane requires H/C. Source retirement is confirmed
before science. Both supplied routes run one reader and one scientific batch;
pasted uses two model exchanges, Upload three. Two identical completion sends
establish replay after storage reopening without another conversation or execution.

now checks its actual body is empty, and the verifier checks the bodyless signature

verification. This corrects the verifier, not production signing. Review also
separated pasted selection=None from its returned record 1, and preserved exact
Upload selection (2,1). Setup/refusal/cancellation cleanup remains the R4 owner.

R6 implemented: shared Hello wire operation, 2026-10-20
-----------------------------------------------------
provider_core.hello owns AnalysisOffering, hello_body, read_hello and HelloAccepted;
ProviderAPI.publish_hello uses the existing signed transport once. Composition
supplies the expected provider and complete current presentation. Existing core
reference, JSON, timestamp, envelope and private-failure owners are reused. Empty
offerings intentionally withdraw the advertised set; no old request is scheduled,
journaled or replayed. Equal accepted_at timestamps remain legal. Acceptance is
an announcement fact then, neither readiness nor proof no newer publisher exists.

Request validation precedes signing/send. Unicode scalar bounds, NUL/surrogate
rejection, at most 64 unique kinds, original order and wire byte ceiling match
the API authority recorded in R6's plan. UTF-8 serialization preserves maximum
legal supplementary Unicode without unnecessary surrogate-escape expansion.
Malformed/foreign successful receipts retain the exact private response/cause.
HTTP/transport failures and cancellation keep their original evidence, with a
safe note that acceptance is unconfirmed, the directory may already have changed,
and no retry occurred. Attempt/grant effect flags are not used for Hello.

Tests first reproduced the missing module through the installed-wheel unit lane.
Focused unit tests then passed (7), local TLS integration (3), installed package
contracts (5) and launcher selectors/grants (7). The TLS witness loads an API-shaped
disposable credential, independently verifies the actual digest/signature and
checks the identity-bound receipt. Lost response sends once; only an explicit new
caller snapshot sends again. Cancellation preserves failure evidence and confirms
socket cleanup. Its test name was corrected after audit: ordinary cancellation
may already settle transport ownership, so it does not claim ownership must remain
until a later retire call. Existing transport tests own unfinished-cleanup cases.

New hierarchical targets are test/unit/provider-core/hello and
test/integration/provider-core/hello. The latter uses the same isolated installed

R8 plan: normal service using the existing lifecycle owners, 2026-10-20
---------------------------------------------------------------------
R8a first exposes a serialized, owner-aware AttemptStore.admission_inventory read.
It returns the existing records only after checking a free logical slot and the
same full-obligation/staging filesystem headroom used by admit. Reuse that exact
calculation; keep authoritative admission/replacement checks in place. Preflight
observes capacity, reserves no physical blocks, changes no records and authorizes
no execution or recovery. Tests first: empty/nonempty read without mutation,
confirmed-record saturation, low space, failed filesystem observation and cancelled
I/O retaining its exact owner. A focused contained target owns these checks.

R8b supplies the NMRNet-owned service admission/loop around existing controller,
API and store owners. Startup checks local obligations/capacity and a bounded
provider-wide inventory before initial current Hello or fresh discovery. Any live
remote row, regardless of analysis kind, holds fresh admission while recovery is
explicit; there is no need to build/deduplicate a partial remote inventory when
the first row already refuses all new work. Empty pagination must terminate within
explicit page and exchange limits; cycles/incomplete traversal never mean empty.
Select the first eligible signed feed row in server order, retain one fresh start
key, then ask a trusted host factory for the existing AttemptController. The factory
owns partial allocations until returning; the service owns the returned controller.
No new execution/replay journal, scientific transformation, command or engine grant.

Use separate Hello transport/task ownership so publication can refresh during a
blocked Attempt. A later publication is a current complete announcement, never an
old queued mutation. On stop, prohibit new admission, drain then cancel/observe
the original invocation under explicit bounds, settle its exact controller and
join Hello before releasing dependencies. Keep held work and independent primary/
cleanup failures; no replacement until retirement is confirmed. Initial policy
may refuse automatic recovery/outages rather than silently retry uncertain work;
continuous-outage recovery and confirmed-record retention need explicit tests/policy
before claiming sustained service. Readiness remains distinct from Hello acceptance.

Tests use actual installed core and local TLS with recording scientific ports:
zero allocation under local/remote/capacity holds; exact discovered source/key into
the existing controller; independent Hello during held execution; idle/discovery/
partial-allocation/active-work stop; failed retirement or lost completion preserves
records and never reruns science. Native/external/deployed qualifications remain
separate. Keep focused routine lanes; repeat expensive native routes only when a
changed boundary requires new evidence. Important U/A and code audits run after
each coherent change through two settled no-signal rounds, then explicit-path commit.

Sources and rationale: NMRPeak 1697dc84dd1d76ae25b04efc8183fd80614000e5,
nmrpeak_provider/provider_process.py:192-214,263-320,445-495 and
attempt_inventory.py:62-116,130-183 establish startup inventory before publication,
bounded traversal and shutdown ownership; its thread/two-lane machinery is not
needed here. SECS 7356c354368f2355d3ab5cef00cf48c67cea9c2c,
src/secs_inference/provider/process.py:38-108 and runtime.py:113-140 establish
paced publication and preservation of independent service failures. Adapt current
async owners; do not copy frozen announcement retries or invent a second scheduler.
Current core attempt_files.py:208-275 owns the capacity arithmetic. Configured
assembly at 33cc2c9 receives an already allocated prediction port, so its existing
inventory check alone cannot prove allocation-after-capacity ordering. These are
the precise gaps R8 addresses; this plan does not claim service implementation.

nothing, allocate nothing and recover nothing. Their page count combined with the

R8c service coordinator: tests-first implementation contract
------------------------------------------------------------
ProviderService owns one retained work task/controller and one independent Hello
task/API, using a fresh configured Hello transport. Explicit ServicePolicy groups
the existing admission policy with finite idle, Hello, drain and settlement waits;
there are no cadence defaults. A synchronous current_announcement callback supplies
the complete current display/description/offerings. Provider identity stays fixed
to configuration. on_started is synchronous and means startup gate plus initial
Hello acceptance, not model/scientific readiness or continuous availability.

run(stop) accepts one invocation: startup gate, initial current Hello, then paced
independent Hello and fresh admission/selection work. A trusted asynchronous
open_attempt factory returns the existing assembled controller. It owns partial
allocations AND interrupted setup I/O until successful return; a clean refusal
must settle both. In particular assemble_attempt's records(owner=None) read belongs
to that factory while setup is outstanding. No service code may adopt arbitrary
owner=None storage I/O as its own. The retained original work task contains the
factory invocation; a late controller is assigned before any stop check, then
settled without running if stop was requested. A task still pending after a bounded
wait remains retained; neither timeout nor cancellation confirms host retirement.

Stop first prevents new allocation, permits a bounded drain, then requests one
cancellation and observes the original task. Controller settlement never overlaps
its run invocation. Observe simultaneous Hello/work failures before claiming clean
stop; preserve primary, independent and cleanup causes with safe outer text.
settle observes original owners only, including service-owned preflight I/O;
caller-owned API/store lifetimes are not closed here. Failed settlement holds the
service/controller and forbids replacement. Startup/publication/discovery outages
stop this first coordinator; blanket retry, stale Hello replay and implicit
recovery are excluded. Explicit outage and retention policy remain 003 work.

Focused local TLS witnesses must allocate the recording scientific worker inside
the trusted factory, prove initial gate/Hello/discovery/allocation/controller order,
zero allocations on holds, refreshed current Hello during blocked execution,
already-stopped and startup/callback failure, stop during late/resistant factory
and active execution, held retirement and independent failures, setup-read
cancellation with original settlement, and lost completion without another run.
This proves coordinator ordering with recording allocations; host provisioning,
native retirement/capacity and actual external/deployed workflow remain separate.
Source rationale is the R8 NMRPeak/SECS ordering/join evidence above and current
core controller.py:81-121,133-166. No new lifecycle implementation belongs here.

R8c implemented locally: service ordering and shutdown, 2026-10-20
----------------------------------------------------------------
ProviderService now uses R8a/R8b and the original core controller. The dedicated
Hello API is constructed from a fresh configured transport, independent of feed/
lifecycle and source/model I/O. Startup gate precedes initial Hello; on_started
means only that these startup observations finished. A serial work task performs
fresh admission/selection before factory handoff, adopting the returned controller
before checking stop. Current empty offerings pause fresh allocation; a withdrawal
during admission/discovery discards the selected in-memory start. Already handed-off
work remains owned until completion or stop. The code makes no scientific-readiness
or cross-process singleton claim, and supplies no engine access or install command.

Stop requests bounded drain, then one cancellation and observation of the original
work task. A resistant factory stays inside that retained task and can later return
a controller which is retired without starting. Controller settlement starts only
after work ends; its own retained task allows bounded observations without issuing
another retirement while the first is pending. Explicit later settlement can retry
a finished failed settlement through that same controller, never its scientific
run or publication command. Store/API cleanup admits only the service's original
I/O owner; setup reads with owner=None remain the factory's responsibility.

ServiceFailed renders owned operation/recovery context and retains an ordered
protected tuple of (operation, original failure). Task callbacks retrieve failures
without letting abandoned waits print private evidence. Mixed cancellation/cleanup
groups stay intact. Factory-owned partial allocation failures remain explicit host/
caller obligations, even after local service tasks and dependencies are settled;
service settlement alone cannot certify their native retirement or safe replacement.
The recording factory retains its allocated ports for that separate cleanup.

R9 connected local service implemented, 2026-10-20
------------------------------------------------
WorkerAllocation publishes only an exact closed role/fresh 32-hex token. A private
staging file is closed before exclusive atomic publication. The original task
retains filesystem work across waiter cancellation. Host acceptance is followed
by allocation-bound channel readiness and bounded prediction runtime metadata;
none of these means loaded-model readiness. Retirement first revokes adoption,
waits for this owner's publication, then uses its original HostRetirement. A failed
exclusive leaf acquisition cannot retire another owner's token. Explicit later
observation can see late host confirmation without another allocation or retirement
publication; earlier failure evidence remains. Local settlement does not itself
confirm native retirement.

provider_workers.sh owns the serial host operations, private-root flock, refusal
of prior evidence, fixed offline role profiles, intent before create, complete
CID-pair admission, observed image/running/isolation/resource posture and exact
stop/inspect/remove through the existing retirement helper. Retirement before or
during create prevents start/handoff. An exact retirement-only leaf fences late
publication without creating a worker or inventing its role. Only known IPC entries
are removed; unexpected grants block confirmation. Caller must stop its controller
before releasing the root. The initial conservative restart policy is a behavioral
refusal until stopped inspection/archive, not automatic recovery or record deletion.

NativeAttemptFactory owns requested allocations/channels and interrupted assembly
reads until returning the existing controller or source pair. Actual metadata,
admitted artifact identities and execution-source identity feed the unchanged
assembly/session. Sources are lazy. Failed setup attempts independent cleanup and

Bounded input interpreter implemented locally, 2026-10-20
========================================================
provider_core.conversation owns the serial transcript, total attempted-turn
budget, admitted assistant envelope, correction mechanics and ordered endpoint
fallback. nmrnet_provider.interpreter owns NMRNet's six tools and prompt. This is
the concrete implementation of interpreter steps 2/4 below, using the already
installed chat carrier and preparation lifetime owner. It adds no dependency,
scientific transform, reference registry, worker protocol or configuration loader.

SMILES uses the exact verified Job byte span and explicit count. Pasted input
uses the exact span and declared format/unit. Upload input inspects membership
and delegates selected bytes/options to the existing acquisition/reader owner.
The chooser cannot replace source text, coordinates, generation settings or
owned budgets. submit consumes only the current prepared reference; the narrow
PreparedReferenceRejected permits correction without catching other ValueError.
InputRejected supplies its safe owned reason; its private native diagnostic is
never model feedback. Operational reader/generator/source/limit failures stop.

Fallback follows confirmed endpoint retirement, preserves completed tool results
and reasoning, and spends the same remaining turn budget. It never reruns a
completed scientific operation. Unidentifiable carriers are operational; malformed
arguments and multiple identifiable calls receive correction without dispatch.
Rejected argument text is discarded before another request; decoded values/keys
are checked for known endpoint credentials. Job text enters as exact plaintext,
avoiding an extra JSON-escaping layer. These are known-secret checks, not a claim
to classify arbitrary private text. Only admitted assistant fields are replayed.

report_input_problem is terminal, attributed and unverified. Its full bounded
explanation remains in core's existing protected failure evidence. The public
message is an owned attribution, not arbitrary model text that might repeat Job
or Upload content. The actual fail_body witness verifies that even a 1500-character
private report produces a publishable failure within the API limit. No science
or second endpoint starts. Model reports cannot become verified invalid_input.
Transient raw endpoint failures stay ordered and private, including failures
before successful fallback; only safe reason/status summaries enter observations.

Secret-free prompt/tool/configuration hashes, route counts and successful operation
names join the existing immutable interpretation observation, prepared identity
and result. The existing lifetime owner retires the chooser before exposing a
candidate; core rejects late reply/dispatch results after retirement and keeps
the original operation plus independent cleanup failures. There is no new journal.

Tests were written before the missing implementation. Focused local TLS witnesses
reach the actual session/result path for exact CCl/N=2, nonzero nm-to-angstrom
pasted conversion, and unlike uploaded XYZ records requested 2,1. They compare
actual posted tools, prompt hash, endpoint routes, exact reader bytes/options,
ordered worker requests and source mappings. A lost model reply then fallback
retains its prepared reference without generating again. Recording scientific
ports do not establish native RDKit, released-model or external LLM behavior.

plan below support shared transcript ownership, narrow correction and separate
exchange/conversation budgets. Core implements the reusable mechanics; NMRNet
keeps its own report precedence and scientific decisions. No sibling/upstream
change, external model call or new acquisition was performed. The user's final
packaging direction retains full runtime dependencies and drops the minimal
installation proposal; note 017 records the decision and rationale.

Interpreter lifetime and observation binding, 2026-10-19
========================================================

InputPreparation now requires explicit chooser.run and chooser.retire operations.
It retains one shielded run task, cancels/joins it during retirement, and shares
one pending close task between preparation and concurrent retirement. Closing
attempts both run settlement and model-I/O retirement independently. No candidate
leaves preparation after submit until model cleanup is confirmed; a phase recheck
also blocks a candidate if retirement began while cleanup was being observed.
Full retirement stops/joins the chooser before settling source/worker/transports,
attempting the independent owners even after a failure. Five recording chooser
fixtures explicitly implement retirement; no optional lookup or no-op fallback
was introduced into production.

A completed failed cleanup observation can be rechecked by a later explicit
retire call, as required by the existing owner/core transport contract. Pending
cleanup remains shared. Rearm never reopens choice or scientific work, never
resends a model request, and retains the prior failure observation. This fixes
both duplicate concurrent close and permanently cached failed-cleanup problems.
Original observed operation errors, cancellation and later cleanup causes remain
available; a failed model cleanup cannot produce a scientific handle.

The trusted chooser may return immutable secret-free observation JSON bytes or
None (no recorded external interpretation). InputPreparation attaches those bytes
once to the existing supplied/generated input value. They describe interpretation
and grant no authority. ResultPlan projects the observation into the public result
and existing protected prepared identity. Existing result capacity bounds raw
observation bytes before UTF-8 decoding; existing whole-result/evidence capacity
then blocks oversized combined identity before scientific dispatch. Mutable
observations are wiring failures. Bytes stay excluded from input repr.

The projection records semantic JSON fields, not original JSON byte identity.
A second strict wire parser/duplicate-key hook was declined: the trusted chooser
owns construction with json.dumps, while untrusted model replies already have
strict wire decoding in chat.py. Model tools must not supply these observation
bytes; the upcoming actual chooser must prove this and its secret-free projection.
No raw model transcript, HTTP response or credential belongs in this observation.

Tests first reproduced missing retirement, dropped observations and unguarded
capacity. Review added deterministic concurrent-run and post-submit cleanup races,

Interpreter HTTPS carrier qualified locally, 2026-10-19
=======================================================

nmrnet_provider/chat.py now supplies ChatEndpointSpec and the thin ChatEndpoint
consumer of installed-core HttpTransport. Composition supplies the complete
canonical HTTPS URL, configuration ID/model/key/optional effort, dedicated TLS
transport, and explicit request/consumed-response byte limits. There is no config
loader, implicit URL/model, environment credential/proxy lookup, HTTP retry, new
dependency or sibling import. The upcoming NMRNet chooser is its specified consumer.

One call serializes/bounds the complete request before I/O and sends one POST.
Core owns actual response consumption, aggregate exchange deadline, cancellation
and retirement. HTTP errors, duplicate/invalid JSON, unsupported completion shape
and interrupted/limited completion remain operational. A limit-stopped reply,
unfinished reply or service filter cannot become a tool choice. Tool-call and
argument admission still belongs to the next conversation owner. Retirement is
explicit, including failure and cancellation, before any endpoint switch.

The immutable endpoint specification hides URL/key in repr. identity contains
safe configuration ID/model and a hash over URL/model/effort/request+response
limits, excluding the key; key rotation does not change it. This is request-
configuration identity, not complete TLS/transport/conversation provenance.
ChatFailed uses safe owned text with the original cause/HTTP response retained
only transiently. Those response bytes may echo secrets or Job text: the chooser
must not serialize them into feedback, result, durable identity or diagnostics.
Record only safe selected reasons/status/route observations through that boundary.

Next interpreter slices: local loop and bounded HTTPS adapter
============================================================

Use nmrnet_provider/interpreter.py for NMRNet tools and provider_core for shared
conversation mechanics and its chat.py carrier. Existing core HttpTransport owns HTTPS,
actual response consumption, aggregate exchange deadlines and retirement. Add no
SDK, generic interpreter framework, ambient proxy/credential discovery or config
file loader. Normal composition will supply explicit ordered endpoint specs,
dedicated transports and TLS trust; no URL/model/key default is authorized here.
No live endpoint selection is inferred from sibling repositories. Local TLS and
scripted behavior tests are independent of later explicitly invoked live calls.

Implement/tests/audit/commit in coherent order:
1. Thin adapter: immutable endpoint facts (keys/URL excluded from repr), explicit
   whole-request and consumed-response bounds, one POST per call, exact tool carrier
   and complete bounded JSON reply. Reuse HttpTransport retirement, including lost
   replies, oversized bodies, trickle deadlines and cleanup uncertainty. Prove the
   actual local TLS wire and safe failure evidence; this is not model behavior.
2. One typed tool loop over existing inspect/prepare_upload/prepare_pasted/
   prepare_smiles plus attributed report_input_problem. Exactly one call per
   turn; malformed arguments and observed InputRejected correct in the same
   transcript. Turn budget spans all endpoints and corrections; InputPreparation's
   existing deadline/work budgets remain authoritative. Successful preparation
   terminally selects immutable owned bytes without another model exchange.
3. Bind retirement and observation: chooser is an explicit retirement obligation,
   including uncertain cancellation cleanup. Attach immutable secret-free
   interpretation bytes once after trusted chooser.run to the existing supplied/
   generated input variants. None means no recorded external interpretation; it
   does not establish a scripted origin. Model tools cannot set this metadata.
   ResultPlan projects it into public result and existing prepared identity before
   scientific dispatch, using existing capacity owners rather than a new journal.
4. Qualify the real chooser with all three input routes, same-transcript correction,
   untrusted instructions, stale/cross-Attempt references and source-backed oracles.
   Real configured-model behavior and deployed API/GUI remain separate exits.

Operational endpoint fallback preserves the transcript, completed tool results,
references and remaining budgets. Confirm failed transport retirement first;
never automatically replay a successful tool effect. A model-authored input-problem
report is terminal and attributed, not verified bad input and not grounds to try
another endpoint. Parser/generator crashes, output limits, aggregate work exhaustion,
source/transport outages and uncertain cleanup are not repairable input failures.
Do not string-match UploadFailed or catch all ValueError: any later correctable
source-choice classification belongs at the existing core source owner. Retain
safe endpoint/model IDs, prompt/tool/config identities and actual route/failures;
never API keys or raw transcripts. Request material/metadata disclosure and live
call authority must be explicit in the eventual invocation lane.

Generation capacity failures become informative Job failures, 2026-10-19
======================================================================

NMRNetSession now maps propagated ConformerShortfall and ConformerOutputLimit to
distinct operational AnalysisFailure codes, using their existing safe messages
with requested/actual counts and the blocked operation. Neither is invalid_input;
neither creates a handle, invokes prediction or submits partial conformers. Only
these typed native outcomes and the existing InputRejected are normalized.
PreparationRetirementFailed and cancellation still propagate unchanged: uncertain
cleanup must not become an ordinary generation outcome. A late failure after
retirement starts cannot select a Job outcome.

smiles_protocol.py::conformer_evidence extracts the existing wire-overflow summary
for reuse by shortfall evidence: exact native provenance text and diagnostic,
ordered actual IDs, request/stereo byte lengths and digests. The terminal binding
adds the original verified Job fingerprint, requested/actual counts and provenance
digest. Output-limit retains coordinates_available=false from its admitted reply;
allocation identity stays with the transport owner. No coordinate requests or
stereo payloads enter protected failure evidence. Failed-Job evidence uses core's
existing capacity owner; an overflow preserves the full document and exact typed
failure in PreparationEvidenceFailed, without clipping or a false terminal success.

Review considered adding a wrapper to carry the selected byte span on failed
generation. This was declined: the failed, nonexecuted operation needs bounded
causal values (Job fingerprint, source SHA, N, settings and actual outcome), and
has no span-dependent consumer or replay path. These facts identify its input
values, not the selected occurrence or independent authorization. Successful
generated input still retains its exact source span through the existing owner.
This decision avoids adding mirrored selection state/error taxonomy solely to
make a failed operation resemble a successful result; no coordinate archive is
promised or introduced. Failed-operation occurrence history is not retained.

Exact Job selection for SMILES, 2026-10-19
=========================================

submission.py::select_job_smiles requires provider_core.job_input.VerifiedJobInput
and explicit start_byte/end_byte/requested_count. It slices the original immutable
specification using a half-open UTF-8 byte span, then uses the existing pure
smiles.py::require_smiles_source token/count admission. No replacement source text,
default N, number-word parser, native parser or allocation is accepted here.
SelectedSmilesSource freezes Job ref, original input fingerprint, offsets, N and
the exact slice; its digest is derived from those bytes and source is absent from
repr. It is ordinary data, not a credential or independent proof of prose intent.
The conversation owner must construct it from the verified input in its own flow;
a later consumer cannot treat a freely constructed instance as authorization.

This deliberately separates provenance from interpretation. The interpreter owns
which SMILES/count the instructions request, including missing/ambiguous N and
authorized corrections. Byte offsets establish where text came from, not what the
commissioner meant. The normative fixture says "three" and supplies selected N=3;
there is no new restriction to decimal-number prose. The generation owner must
not silently reduce N, reseed, or generate again when a reference is submitted.

Tests first reproduced the absent selector. Six structured installed-core tests
now cover multibyte prefixes, exact whitespace/source digest, repeated-token
selection, frozen counts, unverified input, offset/count type and range rejection,
split UTF-8 and private-text-safe diagnostics. New test/integration/provider-core/
smiles is a focused child of the existing offline installed-package lane; its
launcher checks retain the same read-only/no-network/no-engine grants. No new
fixture mount, image or dependency is needed. Generation retirement, selected-batch
binding, diagnostic retention and submission remain the next implementation work.

handle reuse. This proves lifecycle ordering with recording science; actual model

Bounded evidence for generated output limits, 2026-10-19
=======================================================

The previous protocol progress identified a presumed need for durable oversized
failed coordinates. Rechecking this plan's retention contract (Prepared bytes are
Attempt-local; Failed Attempts retain safe causal evidence) rejected that extra
requirement. It would add storage/transport machinery without an accepted result
or recovery consumer. No larger frame, arbitrary smaller N limit or payload archive
is needed to carry this failure honestly.

The complete 2 MiB frame limit remains unchanged. A collection that fits returns
exact requests/stereo/provenance, including a typed full partial on native shortfall.
If that envelope overflows, conformer_reply instead returns conformer_output_limit
with exact native provenance and diagnostics plus ordered actual conformer IDs,
request/stereo byte lengths and SHA-256 digests. It explicitly marks coordinates
unavailable. The receiver raises operational ConformerOutputLimit, never source
rejection, successful input or a shortfall that pretends to retain coordinates.
No automatic smaller N, prefix publication, inference or hash-based replay follows.
The existing Attempt private-evidence owner will retain this bounded diagnostic;
there is no separate coordinate archive. Transport failure remains uncertain and
does not guarantee even this summary reached its receiver.

Fourteen focused protocol cases cover exact frame fit/+1 overflow, independent
digests/lengths, incomplete native generation that also exceeds output capacity,
and tampered summary fields. Large whitespace-padded JSON requests are authored
transport fixtures, not native ETKDG observations or scientific capacity proof.
The native success roundtrip stays the independent ordinary-path witness.
All 14 protocol and 11 native checks passed; changed lint/format passed. Code,
science and operations/error review sustained two unchanged clear passes after
correcting historical wording. The full native provenance stays private alongside
the existing failure evidence, avoiding a second selectively copied metadata schema.

Initial SMILES protocol admission, 2026-10-19 (historical e256ed6)
================================================================

nmrnet_provider/smiles_protocol.py now encodes the exact UTF-8 source/count and
frozen native collection. UTF-8 JSON text preserves original bytes without base64
expansion. No work-budget fields are accepted from a request. The receiver checks
caller-owned source SHA/count/settings, ordered unique actual IDs, executable
angstrom requests and complete source/runtime/stereo evidence with valid atom
references. It does not reparse SMILES, add H or recompute stereochemistry.
Source correspondence is a producer qualification obligation; internal reference
checks do not prove that the source was chemically interpreted correctly.

The encoder derives success/shortfall from the one requested/returned count pair;
there is no independently supplied disposition flag. A valid shortfall reconstructs
ConformerShortfall with the exact full private partial, never successful input.
Malformed replies preserve their decoded contents privately in WorkerResultRejected.
The complete frame, including escaped metadata and diagnostics, was admitted before
returning an encodable reply. That revision retained overflow only in process.
The bounded operational evidence policy above supersedes this initial behavior;
the current receiver gets counts, provenance, diagnostics and payload digests,
with coordinates explicitly unavailable and no successful prefix.

The explicit make test/unit/smiles-protocol lane uses the established
tests/unit/normative and tests/unit/adversarial split. Its authored protocol data
are not a native scientific reference. The pinned native generator lane separately
checks that a real generated collection survives framing/admission unchanged and
that decoding cannot regenerate it.

worker exit. Review of tools/worker_qualification.sh / tools/inference_native.sh found the
existing 64-block output file limit (64 KiB in the actual Bash launch) cannot retain a >2 MiB generated
collection in stderr, and geometry qualification does not preserve that capture
as payload evidence. Do not silently repurpose retirement-control directories.

capacity proof. The retention-contract review above rejected that extra failed-
coordinate preservation requirement. The existing 2 MiB frame remains authoritative;
the current operational failure summary fits that channel without a new archive.

Verified Upload selection through one retained batch result, 2026-10-19
