cumulative HTTP request/byte/deadline limits. A separate explicit read budget is
charged at parser entry, including rejected records, rather than on acquisition.
The overall preparation timeout includes chooser waits. No external model is called.

The complete inspect/acquire coroutine is retained across cancellation, because
acquisition spans membership refresh, grant issuance and store transfer. Retirement
cancels and settles it before closing its reusable transports. A phase check before
handoff prevents late bytes from being cached after retirement. Reader allocation
is likewise retained as a shielded task: cancellation cannot discard a late client
and exact host-retirement handle. The factory remains responsible for partially
created resources before returning that pair. Every reader closes, confirms host
retirement and observes local closure before a reference is issued or replacement
is allowed. Uncertain cleanup keeps the owner and blocks replacement/submission.

existing scientific session; it does not grant engine authority or declare states.

Whole-result planning for ordered geometries, 2026-10-19
========================================================

ResultPlan is the existing scientific result owner, now generalized to a nonempty
ordered tuple of PreparedGeometry and an equally ordered tuple of worker outcomes.
It freezes one reproduction envelope and reserves its complete JSON capacity
before execution. There is no second batch planner or per-entry publication.
Each entry retains its selection-order geometry ID, original source record/atom
mapping, request hash, explicit conversion, coordinates and supplied-H coverage.
Repeated geometries stay separate; no equivalence check or averaging is added.

Completion checks each outcome against that geometry's request hash, required
H/C artifact subset, runtime and ordered eligible atom rows. Later failure returns
no bytes. Protected raw outcomes remain caller-owned; the planner does not execute
or retain partial successes. The compact identity projects ordered source/request
facts and required_elements from the same frozen envelope, excluding coordinates
and raw text. Required does not claim that a model has already loaded.

The existing v1 envelope already contains a geometries collection. Warning records
gain an additive geometry_id, including singleton results; their existing fields
and top-level location remain. Each actual observation is associated with its
outcome, never merged into a claim about independent loads. The API treats this
product as opaque; no closed warning schema or deployed client contract was found.
Stored terminal bytes still replay exactly, without rebuilding the result. No
conditional singleton compatibility path or new schema is introduced.

The direct NMRNetSession and real-worker parity caller use singleton tuples with
the same planner; the scientific worker still handles one geometry. This slice
does not enable batch execution, Upload acquisition or an interpreter. Next extend
the existing worker's one-use preparation handle to the whole collection: admit
every request before any checkpoint/scaler load, execute sequentially, and retain
prior outcomes privately if later inference fails. Then connect actual JobUploads
and the binding-owned interpreter to this same result and lifecycle owner.

Five initial batch witnesses were authored first; four failed because only a
single PreparedGeometry was accepted. The extended suite covers different and
repeated XYZ records (2,1,3), original line mapping, missing/extra/reordered or bad
later outcomes, head/warning isolation, aggregate-only overflow, and a complete
escaped envelope at capacity with extreme finite shifts and both warning kinds.
The missing-model-selection audit finding was reproduced in three failing cases
and fixed at plan construction, before scientific dispatch.

The core evidence limit remains owned by ProtectedEvidence, constructed by the
session before worker.prepare. A synthetic 112-geometry, 128-O-atom-per-geometry

MOL2 reader-process and container binding, 2026-10-19
====================================================

The existing private reader now carries MOL2 with the same bounded source and
ordered-selection request used by XYZ/SDF. Reply admission preserves original
positive, unique atom IDs through 2^53-1 rather than imposing contiguous MOL rows.
Whole-source hash, units and conversion facts come from the caller's exact bytes
and options. The reader owns correspondence; the receiver does not parse again.

Three process witnesses first failed without MOL2 routing, then passed: different
records in requested order, nonconsecutive IDs with explicit nm conversion, and
selected-later unsupported types rejecting without a successful prefix. Additional
receiver tests reject duplicate, zero, oversized or boolean atom IDs, false position
kinds and changed selected record order. The fixed host lane
test/integration/provider-geometry-worker/mol2 selects water record 2 then
chloromethane record 1 against the existing independent expected.json. A negative
oracle witness substitutes record 1's coordinates into record 2 and must fail.

The lane reuses the existing CID/image/profile admission, HostRetirement and IPC
cleanup. Only the controller mounts the source and expected data. The pure worker
has no RDKit/Torch, input-file mount, network, engine, checkpoints or credentials.

and includes the MOL2 fixture only for its actual case. These are fixed reader
and retirement witnesses, not Upload/interpreter, batch inference or deployment
qualification. The direct-reader limits below describe commit b087ba1; this

MOL2 coordinate projection, 2026-10-19
=====================================

The selected implementation is pure nmrnet_provider/mol2.py, sharing existing
decode_source/prepare_rows/unit admission with other readers. NMRNet consumes
elements and XYZ; no native graph/charge perception is needed to obtain those
fields. This supersedes the tentative RDKit-MOL2 path in 015/016 without weakening
source preservation or worker/container qualification. The source is retained by
whole-file digest, original record ordinal and original integer ATOM IDs in file
order. No bond, charge, isotope/stereo or alternate-attribute interpretation,
molecular-equivalence check, atom reordering or H completion is performed.

Tripos, Tripos Mol2 File Format, SYBYL 7.1 (Mid-2005), printed/PDF pp. 3, 12 and
53-54 owns section framing, ATOM rows and concrete SYBYL type interpretation:
https://zhanggroup.org/DockRMSD/mol2.pdf#page=3
https://zhanggroup.org/DockRMSD/mol2.pdf#page=12
https://zhanggroup.org/DockRMSD/mol2.pdf#page=53
The complete concrete dotted vocabulary has 27 entries, represented by small
per-element suffix sets; unknown suffixes never become elements by prefix guessing.
Two real sulfur spelling aliases, S.o/S.o2, are explicit extensions supported by
MDAnalysis 2.9.0 SYBYL2SYMB and Open Babel 3.1.1 MOL2Format::ReadMolecule:
https://docs.mdanalysis.org/2.9.0/_modules/MDAnalysis/guesser/tables.html
https://github.com/openbabel/openbabel/blob/openbabel-3-1-1/src/formats/mol2format.cpp#L269-L272
The manual's extracted text uses uppercase O; no rendered-image verification was
possible. The independent published mappings agree with uppercase forms and also
support the two lowercase aliases. This is not general case folding. Bare element
spellings beyond the table are a declared extension; the existing request parser
and later model dictionary own usable element admission. Pseudo/query types and
D/T labels request correction rather than creating/removing a supplied nucleus.

requirements/preparation.md retains the complete reference pages/sections and
precise native research chain (RDKit issue 9421 -> PR 9417 -> released changelog),
with unavailable advisory, older-source and binary/source identity limits. It does
not mistake Open Babel advisories for RDKit issues or clear the separate V3000
block. Avoiding the native MOL2 graph parser is a product-boundary choice, not
evidence that the retained toolkit is safe or unsafe for that path.

Framing covers the whole bounded source; section markers start with @ in column 1.
Only selected ATOM rows/types/counts are parsed. BOND/SUBSTRUCTURE/attribute content
is opaque. Selected ATOM rows must occupy one physical line; unsupported wrapped
rows request correction. A failed selected record returns no successful prefix;
unselected unsupported chemistry does not block an otherwise readable selection.
Bounds reuse 1 MiB source bytes, 128 records and 128 selected atoms/record. Source
IDs are positive integers through 2^53-1 so JSON consumers preserve their exact
identity; this is an explicit transport bound, not a Tripos integer-width claim.
Significant digit count is checked before integer conversion. Source units remain
the caller's explicit assertion; conversion uses the shared scientific constants.

Tests were authored and failed before the module existed. The maintained offline
test/unit/provider/mol2 lane includes normative/adversarial groups and independent
expectations for all 27 dotted types plus two aliases, original corpus order/H and
different/repeated records, arbitrary nonconsecutive IDs, conversion, metadata and
LF/CRLF hashes, selected suffix failure, section/count/ID/coordinate errors and
resource limits. Existing fixture expectations were not regenerated. This direct
reader is not yet the private-worker, Upload/interpreter or deployed offering.
Focused evidence: all 14 direct-reader checks pass, plus the pure-reader launcher
regression (both PDB/MOL2) and two guard/hierarchical-Make checks. The full declared
dotted vocabulary and two observed aliases are exercised without importing RDKit
or Torch in the lightweight checker. Changed Python lint/format, shell
syntax and whitespace checks pass. No native/model run, package acquisition,
upstream/sibling edit, interpreter call or deployment was performed in this slice.
Code, science/provenance and operations/error auditors each returned no actionable
signal in two final unchanged passes. Accepted findings enforce RTI column 1,
document and test the two actual sulfur aliases, and refuse Python-only numeric
digit separators. The underscore witness first reproduced 1_0 becoming 10.0;
the reader now requires decimal/exponent tokens before shared finite admission.
Expected input errors identify record and ATOM row without echoing source values.

PDB coordinate reader, 2026-10-19
=================================================

The actual reader nmrnet_provider/pdb.py extracts fixed-column coordinates without
RDKit. NMRNet needs no PDB graph; a native projection would require qualifying a
second parser's filtering, graph handling and atom correspondence. This replaces
the prospective native-PDB implementation wording in 016, while retaining its
source-preservation and real worker/container proof requirements. No present-version
RDKit behavior is asserted from older source code. Real native evidence remains
required for MOL/MOL2 and SMILES where those implementations use RDKit.

Primary authorities, verified 2026-10-19 by the independent format investigator:
https://www.wwpdb.org/documentation/file-format-content/format33/sect9.html#MODEL
  Serial columns 11-14 and paired ENDMDL delimit explicit models.
https://www.wwpdb.org/documentation/file-format-content/format33/sect9.html#ATOM
https://www.wwpdb.org/documentation/file-format-content/format33/sect9.html#HETATM
  Atom serial 7-11, atom name 13-16, altLoc 17, residue 18-27, X/Y/Z 31-54,
  element 77-78. Element columns own identity; atom names do not guess elements.
https://www.wwpdb.org/documentation/file-format-content/format33/sect9.html#ENDMDL
https://www.wwpdb.org/documentation/file-format-content/format33/sect11.html#END
  A complete END-terminated entry is the supported subset. No hidden trailing
  records, nested/duplicate/unclosed models, mixed implicit/explicit atom regions
  or nonblank unused framing fields are accepted.

Selection keeps source order for all, caller order for explicit model serials, and
independent source atom serials in original row order. Without MODEL, record 1 is
the sole source-block ordinal, not a claimed declared model serial. No cross-model
equivalence, alignment, H completion, isotope conversion or connectivity parsing
is performed. Explicit element fields are required. Atom serials must be unique
across all alternatives in a selected model: source references use serials, not
ambiguous occurrences. Identity/site metadata is therefore admitted for those rows;
coordinate/element values are parsed only for the selected alternate. All atom
semantics of unselected models remain opaque; framing is always checked.

One explicit printable nonblank ASCII altLoc label must resolve every alternate
site; common blank-location sites survive once. Missing labels, duplicate labels,
blank/alternate conflicts at one site and conflicting residue/record naming request
correction. Uniform label selection is our supported subset, not a wwPDB mandate;
independent per-residue choices need a separately selected structure. Occupancy
never selects. Source site identity is chain/residue number/insertion code/atom
name; conflicting residue names or record kinds cannot create duplicate sites.

PDB's documented convention is angstrom. The required source_unit argument follows
MOL/SDF/XYZ and permits a user-stated nonstandard-unit assertion, retaining its
factor/reference. It never estimates units from bond size. The future interpreter
must carry evidence for overriding the format convention. A proposal to forbid
all overrides was declined against the maintainer's explicit-rescaling direction;
a second optional MODEL identity was declined in favor of the documented ordinal.

Tests were written and failed before the module existed. The maintained offline
test/unit/provider/pdb lane exercises original corpus serial/element/H order, A/B
selection with perturbed occupancy, independent nonconsecutive models with different
coordinates/atom sets, explicit units, whole-source LF/CRLF hashes, unselected values,
private-safe selected failures, framing and byte/model/atom bounds. The existing
repeated-model fixture is preserved; the new unlike-model witness is independently
authored in the test instead of regenerating expected data with this parser.
At 1b2ba0c this direct-reader evidence did not advertise PDB Uploads or prove a
private-worker/host-retirement binding; the dated follow-up above supersedes that
worker gap. MOL2, SMILES and the Upload/interpreter binding remain open.
Final focused evidence: 14 reader tests pass through the maintained offline lane;
two launcher regressions and two relevant guard/hierarchical-Make checks pass.
Changed Python lint/format, shell syntax and whitespace checks pass. Sources and
existing expected fixtures were preserved. No RDKit/model run, acquisition, sibling
edit, external interpreter request or deployment was performed in this slice.
Code, science/provenance and operations/error auditors each completed two final
passes without actionable signal. Accepted corrections reject nonblank framing
tails and DEL in inspected columns, admit printable alternate labels, and leave
unselected alternate coordinate/element values opaque. The request to permit
ambiguous duplicate source serials was declined for the correspondence reason
above. No unrelated expected-fixture regeneration or molecular validation was added.

Retain selected input before inference, 2026-10-19

The original Job fingerprint cannot identify the geometry chosen by an interpreter.

Missing identity, a refused/uncertain write or a changed active state prevents
dispatch. Existing write settlement, claim ownership and retirement remain the
owners; no journal, cache or new recovery executor was added.

ResultPlan.prepared_identity projects the frozen admitted envelope into ordered
request hashes, whole-source digest/record/atom mapping, method/unit/factor/reference,
execution identity and admitted model/runtime facts. Coordinates, Job prose and
private warnings are excluded. This records the candidate selected before dispatch,
not proof that the worker consumed it or loaded those artifacts; returned worker
evidence still supplies that later proof. NMRNetSession returns the exact handle it
will consume. A copied handle with identical evidence cannot execute.

Identity-bearing acknowledgements use strict acknowledged.v2. Historical v1 records
retain their exact field set and original byte bound; entered v1 records still have
unknown candidate identity and cannot rerun. Terminal-v1 envelopes retain either
acknowledgement version without changing exact publication bytes. The existing
262144-byte evidence bound is reused, rather than inventing a smaller unmeasured
batch cap. Store admission reserves both input identity and independent terminal
evidence before effects. Maximum-record witnesses combine both maximum payloads
and worst-case escaped identity-schema text; neither payload is truncated.

Tests first exposed absent identity/mandatory dispatch enforcement. Maintained
offline lanes test/unit/provider-core/prepared-identity and

cases. Each selected pattern must contain tests. The direct NMRNet signed-TLS test
compares the retained candidate request hash with the published result; scripted

pre-dispatch identity prerequisite, not authenticated Upload/interpreter binding,
whole-batch inference, startup service orchestration or deployed qualification.

Focused evidence: 65 installed-core unit checks and 21 installed-core integration
checks passed after the final boundary/capacity corrections. Another 21 offline

loading only the pinned proxy image into local Docker, with routing owned by its

Pre-download advisory review: Python 3.12.12 has later security patch releases.
The 3.12.15 urllib fix concerns urllib.request.HTTPPasswordMgr, whereas this proxy
uses urllib.parse.urlsplit without HTTP credential handling. That scoped distinction
supports retaining the declared pin for this operation, not an image-wide safety
claim. Sources consulted for exact scope and rationale (2026-10-19):
https://www.python.org/downloads/release/python-31215/
https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/155694
https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-2v69-2w5x-455j
The pinned image's Alpine base also predates security maintenance in 3.22.6:
https://alpinelinux.org/posts/Alpine-3.21.8-3.22.6-3.23.6-3.24.2-released.html
This bootstrap does not refresh that base or establish that every image advisory
is inapplicable; a runtime refresh needs its own reviewed pin and qualification.

client: current workers require the original registry index-digest reference.

exported archive would not restore that digest reference. Primary source checked
by the delegated precedent reviewer:
https://github.com/moby/moby/blob/docker-v29.8.1/daemon/internal/image/tarexport/load.go#L1047-L1067
https://github.com/moby/moby/blob/docker-v29.8.1/daemon/internal/image/tarexport/load.go#L1211-L1219
This is a bounded provisioning exception, not a new image transfer framework.

checker, covering

and U16/U19/U22 (provenance/privacy). A proposed content-trust concern was withdrawn:
Docker permits explicit-digest pulls without tag-signature lookup; no shared helper
change was justified. Source: https://docs.docker.com/engine/security/trust/

Supplied SDF record selection, 2026-10-19
--------------------------------------
The direct SDF reader now accepts all records or an ordered unique tuple of
positive existing record numbers. It preserves supplied atoms/H/order/coordinates
through the qualified V2000 MOL reader, including its explicit unit conversion.
Source correspondence uses original record numbers and one-based MOL atom rows;
source_sha256 names the complete original SDF bytes, including unselected records
and associated data. Records are not checked for molecular equivalence. No atoms
are generated, added or reordered; selected records follow the requested order
and are never combined into an inferred molecular ensemble.
Selected failure returns no partial tuple. Input rejections retain safe record
context and private native diagnostics; operational exceptions keep their original
identity/type/chain, with a record-context note.

The shared 1 MiB original-source bound, UTF-8 admission and 128-record cap precede
native parsing. Global record framing is checked; unselected structure blocks and
data items are not sent through a native reader. Selected data headers/blank
terminators are framed, while data values stay opaque and outside prediction.
Selected V3000 and non-3D records remain blocked by the MOL owner. Each record
requires a closing delimiter; unframed trailing structure/data is rejected.

Primary format reference and rationale are in sdf.py:
MDL CTFile Formats, October 2003, chapter 6, printed pp. 39-41, original-spec mirror
https://www.daylight.com/meetings/mug05/Kappler/ctfile.pdf#page=40
The helper inspected printed p. 40 to adjudicate two independent reviewers'
exact-delimiter proposal: the specification says a line beginning with four dollar
signs terminates a record. Prefix recognition is therefore retained; exact-line-only
recognition would change the cited grammar. A focused witness covers annotated
delimiters and leading-space data values, preserving selection order and digest.

Tests first: the maintained SDF lane failed on the absent module before reader
implementation. make test/integration/sdf-reader now passes 17 checks in the
admitted RDKit image, offline/nonroot/read-only with bounded scratch and no engine
socket. The launcher regression covers both MOL/SDF read-only source/fixture
grants. Existing independent expected.json and authored mixed-record, supplied
butane-conformer and invalid-suffix fixtures are consumed without regenerating
expected outputs. Reader checks cover different/repeated molecules, supplied H
and carbon-only records, units, source identity, ordered selection, invalid selected
suffix, unselected malformed/V3000 records, inclusive 1 MiB/128-record boundaries
and over-limit rejection. Successful geometry cases exercise real native parsing;
diagnostics and operational failures are injected to check their boundary handling,
not claimed as observed native failure outcomes. Boundary checks demonstrate reader
admission/work bounds, not scientific prediction capacity.

The named hierarchical lane uses the same admitted immutable geometry image as
MOL; a missing requested test module now fails instead of silently discovering
zero tests. Changed-file lint/format, shell syntax and whitespace checks passed.
Independent science/U/A and ordinary code/containment review found no remaining
actionable issue in repeated unchanged-behavior passes. README distinguishes direct
reader qualification from public submission. At that revision SDF/MOL dispatch
through the geometry wire remained unfinished, alongside authenticated Upload
acquisition, other native formats and SMILES conformer generation. Neither that
slice nor the diagnostic slice closed 003.

and text/XYZ line numbers. Native source positions must be exactly 1 through the
returned atom count, within each selected record; atom-map fields are not source
IDs. It checks selected source record numbers/order, reconstructs the whole-source

native parsing correspondence: the reader owns that scientific interpretation.

remain at coordinate_conversion, using its precise NIST/CODATA references.

contiguous returned IDs; explicit selections additionally have a caller-known
count/order. Review declined a worker-declared total (same authority, no independent

Implementation progress, 2026-10-18
===================================

Coordinator implemented the independent input foundation while the shared-provider
integration seam is investigated. No provider offering, worker or upload route is
enabled. Stage 1 and full 2a are not complete.

The first foundation (d386d50) in nmrnet_provider/input_geometry.py reuses parse_request for structural admission,
keeps direct angstrom JSON bytes, and converts one contiguous element/X/Y/Z table
from explicit angstrom/nm to angstrom. Immutable bytes/tuples retain source digest,
source positions and their kind (JSON atom index versus table line number). It
does not admit dictionary vocabulary, load models, infer units, generate geometry
or, at that commit, provide bohr/XYZ/file readers. NIST SP811 Appendix B.8 is
cited beside the exact nm conversion with its rationale. Provider modules enter
existing guard/lint/cache/semantic inventories and read-only checker mounts.

then passed through make test/unit/provider in the pinned offline Python image.

from moving model.py's shared guard before standard-library imports; the existing
guard check had exposed that preexisting ordering violation. A focused lint probe
also exposed 14 preexisting import-policy findings in model.py/predict.py. Pure
shared imports moved to module scope; necessary lazy scientific imports retain
their admission ordering with narrow explained exceptions. Changed files pass
pinned Ruff lint/format. Two real-Make target ownership tests also pass. No full repository or real-model suite
was run for this foundation. The test runner's old two-mount assertion was stale;

Independent input/science and inventory/containment reviews corrected H/C unit
guidance, row-location naming, independent package-mount fixtures and optional

the internal prepared dataclass was declined: current builders already admit exact
bytes and construct immutable tuples; this record is not a wire admission token.

Second internal reader slice, 2026-10-18: plain XYZ now accepts explicitly selected
records (or all), preserving their requested order, original record numbers and
atom line positions. Different molecules are accepted; there is no same-molecule
check. Pasted XYZ requires one frame. Selected malformed records reject the entire
selection. Unselected records require readable framing, not valid coordinates.
The reader caps original bytes at 1 MiB and source frames at 128; the latter is an
internal work bound, not a measured or advertised service batch capacity. Extended
XYZ metadata is rejected, not interpreted as plain coordinates. No bonds inferred.

Bohr conversion uses the CODATA 2022 central value 0.529177210544 angstrom/bohr,
retaining factor, reference and source unit with each prepared geometry. It is not
an exact physical constant: standard uncertainty is 8.2e-11 angstrom/bohr. Sources
and rationale are beside prepare_rows and the XYZ reader: CODATA 2022 table XXXII,
https://physics.nist.gov/cuu/pdf/JPCRD2022CODATA.pdf ; plain XYZ count/comment/rows,
https://openbabel.org/docs/FileFormats/XYZ_cartesian_coordinates_format.html .
Plain XYZ needs no graph interpretation or RDKit dependency; a bounded strict
reader avoids changing the qualified inference environment for this route.

Focused evidence: 19 provider tests pass in the offline container, including
ordered selections, record identity, malformed selected/unselected coordinates,
nonfinite/overflow rejection, record limits, private-source-safe errors and bohr
conversion provenance. The changed launcher-selector regression and changed-file
Ruff lint pass. This does not qualify a worker, native readers, Upload access,
model execution or the end-to-end provider. Those remain required by this plan.
Review removed a test that would have implied silently accepting contradictory
unit evidence. XYZ comments are opaque to this deterministic reader; resolving
such evidence belongs to the interpreter before it selects an explicit unit.
Independent preparation/science and inventory/containment reviews found no further
actionable issues after these corrections. This is reader-slice review only.

Purpose and completion
======================

Call this stage "Read supplied 3D structures or generate conformers
from SMILES." Read one pasted geometry or selected records from an uploaded
structure file, converting established units to angstroms. Generate estimated
coordinates with RDKit from a SMILES molecular description. Keep each supplied
atom linked to its original source record, and retain the generated coordinates
and settings for SMILES. Both paths use the existing NMRNet input checks before
prediction. The umbrella word
"preparation" alone does not tell readers which operation is involved.
Source review and context-isolated reader teach-backs distinguished these two
operations; a misleading suggestion of record selection within pasted input was
corrected. This is a terminology change, not new runtime qualification.

A commissioner selects the NMRNet offering and submits an analysis request. The
provider interprets it, reads supplied geometries or generates conformers from
SMILES, checks NMRNet's input requirements, and predicts atom-associated H/C shifts
for publication through the API. Developers, operators,
commissioners and receivers must receive useful safe causes when this fails.

003 closes when the agreed user workflow and failure/restart recovery work through
a real contained local API deployment and its actual GUI surfaces, with portable
evidence and operating instructions. Startup, scripted peers or existing scientific
tests alone cannot close it. Production activation is separate.

Starting science is closed 002 at revision
7e320505fdbee228e312778032b144cc14ef38b9. Its packet is
artifacts/prediction-cpu-float32.json, SHA-256
3054ace5fb17b824e114c65c25e24389eca7551e38a0aece7f347f5b98f5fa75.
It qualifies CPU float32, the selected fold-0 pair, four fixtures, 137 outputs,
64 historical positions and real 128-atom capacity. It does not establish general
chemical accuracy, ensemble accuracy, inferred bonds or archive-wide unit provenance.

Why the plan changed and sources
===============================

The earlier coordinate-JSON-only plan wrongly removed interpretation. Its U/A
reviews checked constraints inside that scope without adequately challenging the
product workflow. Interpretation of supplied input and generation of missing
geometry are different responsibilities. An interpreter belongs in this offering;
geometry generation needs its own scientific qualification if the product needs it.

notes/014_provider_workflow_comparison_20261018.txt records the actual sibling-code
comparison, pinned revisions, source paths/sections, rationale and rejected findings.
Its W01-W12 findings govern this revision. Aliases M/P/S/I/G below refer to that
source register. Earlier plan/review records remain historical in commits
5e836c3247d1d7705d2b070c19cdada7530be706 and
bfaa5a85fa73fe72e4dc836212ee3942483001b5; they do not certify this revised workflow.

Use these as behavioral precedents for the local package in 017; do not import
the sibling package as the active runtime owner:
- M:pyproject.toml, magnet_provider/analysis_contract.py and
  analysis_implementation.py: packaged core owns lifecycle; binding owns analysis.
- M:magnet_provider/job_input.py and runner_session.py: verified source identity,
  bounded exchange and determinate versus unobserved outcomes.
- M:G01 processor/binding and P:attempt_lifecycle.py/input_interpreter.py, at the
  full paths in 014 W01-W03: interpreted and structured routes converge at admission.
- S:src/secs_inference/provider/{analysis_run,execution,diagnostics}.py and
  M:magnet_provider/{journal_inspect,journal_archive}.py: distinct scientific,
  lifecycle, diagnostics and held-work recovery owners.
- I:nmr_api/public/job_create_request.py, job_specification.py,
  provider/execution_attempt_outcome.py and provider/http_contract.py: exact source
  text, the 65,536 UTF-8 byte specification bound, terminal bytes and server expiry.
- G:app/src/features/workspace/ColumnExecutionAttempt.tsx,
  result-presentation/ResultPresentation.tsx and generic-result-preview.ts:
  actual status/failure and JSON/text preview/download consumers. Offering text
  in ProgressiveText.tsx does not promise clickable Markdown.

Keep precise references and non-obvious adaptation rationale at the owning code
or declaration. Full history belongs in notes/evidence, not repeated comments.

Commissioner-to-receiver workflow
=================================

1. Publish a truthful offering and let an authorized project select it.
   Choose an existing active analysis kind when its semantics fit; a different
   implementation does not automatically need a new kind. Freeze implementation,
   runner and result identities. Use a dedicated provider identity or publish its
   complete offering set: hello replaces that provider's snapshot (014 W05).

2. Admit the authenticated Job and interpret the commissioner's request.
   Preserve exact original text and identity. Direct structured coordinate JSON
   may bypass language interpretation through a declared deterministic route.
   Recognizable malformed structured input must not fall through to prose.

   Three routes are required: SMILES plus requested conformer count N; plain-text
   coordinates for one conformer; attached Uploads plus interpreter instructions.
   Uploaded records need not be checked for molecular equivalence. The detailed
   preparation contract below governs these routes. Demonstrate all three, with
   representative requests, scientific inputs and rejection guidance.

3. Produce a bounded collection of source-backed geometries through deterministic
   preparation, then one scientific request per geometry.
   For supplied geometry, extract/select explicit atoms, order, coordinates and
   unit. Prefer selecting an exact representation for parsing over language-model
   transcription of numbers. Prove correspondence to the source, not just schema
   validity. Missing or ambiguous data must produce useful guidance; the language
   model must not invent coordinates, units, atoms or chemical facts.

   SMILES preparation is required and separately qualified. Record conformer count,
   seed, hydrogen policy, source-to-model atom map and actual generated geometry.
   Do not describe it as supplied or experimental geometry. Names/resolvers and
   force-field optimization are outside this slice; no inferred extra services.

4. Execute the admitted coordinate bytes through unchanged predict_request and
   SelectedReleaseModels. Original Job identity and executed-input identity are
   distinct. Direct coordinate JSON retains its bytes; extracted/prepared input
   has a separately identified output, passed unchanged after admission.

   Preserve 002's 1-128 atoms, finite float32 coordinates, dictionary vocabulary,
   duplicate/extra-field rejection, paired artifacts and verified-byte decoding.
   Require exact angstrom even for no-H/C input (002 checks present heads only).
   Conversion to angstroms happens explicitly before admission, with source-unit
   evidence and recorded factor. No hidden scientific repair. Keep original indices; skip
   absent heads; no-H/C input retains explicitly described successful empty output.
   H then C run sequentially and release each head.

5. Publish a validated all-or-nothing result through the existing API owner.
   Preserve unit=ppm and atom_index/element/shift_ppm in each geometry's order. Check full
   eligible coverage, index/element agreement and finite output; no partial H
   success after C failure. Link Job and executed-input identities, atom convention,
   fixed model/runtime and preparation provenance once. Retain safe interpreter
   model/prompt/config identity with the outcome, without secrets or raw prose.
   Freeze envelope/size against API and actual GUI preview/download: recipients
   must see correspondence, units, provenance and limits. No new renderer is needed.
   Return a separate identified prediction per geometry, never an implicit average.
   Predictions are not measurements, simulated spectra or confidence estimates.

Input preparation contract and interpreter loop
===============================================

Evidence and ownership
----------------------
015 records precise RDKit references and M/P/S source pins with rationale. SECS's
InterpretationSession owns inspection, selection and same-conversation correction;
Magnet's InterpretationCapability/interpret and NMRPeak's runner admission provide
typed construction and deterministic rejection feedback. Reuse compatible owners;
do not transplant SECS spectrum/formula rules or build a universal tool framework.
Reader API availability is source evidence, not accepted-format qualification.

Representative requests for the guide and end-to-end fixtures:
- "Predict H/C shifts for SMILES CCl; generate 3 conformers."
- "Coordinates in angstroms, columns element x y z:" followed by the retained
  CH3Cl fixture's actual coordinate table (reuse it, do not invent reference data).
- "Use all records in the attached conformers.sdf; report each separately."
- "Use frame 2 of the attached XYZ; its coordinates are in nm."
The final guide includes complete rights-cleared runnable inputs, actual outputs
and visible correction examples. No implicit default N or selection on ambiguity.

Keep four responsibilities explicit: source access owns authenticated bounded bytes;
format readers/generation own preparation; admission owns executable geometry;
the existing inference callable owns shifts. The interpreter chooses from declared
operations and sees bounded facts/errors. It cannot supply executable code, host
paths, arbitrary URLs, parsers or invented coordinates for uploaded structures.

The shared prepared value is a nonempty ordered collection of geometries. Each
entry has a stable geometry ID, ordered element symbols, coordinates in angstroms,
source identity (Job span or Upload digest and record/frame), source atom identities,
and preparation provenance. Optional bonds support diagnostics only. Do not require
a shared atom array, shared graph, cross-record alignment or molecular-equivalence
test. Each entry must satisfy 002 independently. Use named immutable records at
module boundaries; do not add a separate schema registry or proof-token hierarchy.
Freeze nested values or exact serialized bytes too; a frozen record around mutable
coordinate lists is insufficient. Caller mutation must not change admitted science.

The preparation owner serializes one exact request per geometry for existing
parse_request and dictionary admission; the provider adds the explicit angstrom
requirement even for empty H/C output. Preserve exact source and executed identities
separately. Avoid a second implementation of 002 validation in tools or schemas.
Exception to serialization: the direct coordinate-JSON shortcut retains original
request bytes unchanged and wraps them as one prepared entry. Both cases then use
the same admission and execution path, not separate scientific implementations.
That shortcut requires coordinate_unit exactly "angstrom". Other units receive
explicit correction guidance; conversion belongs to the declared preparation routes,
which create distinct executed bytes and identities, never mutate the shortcut.

Routes and supported formats
----------------------------
SMILES + N: require a source-backed SMILES and positive integer N within configured
limits. Parse in contained RDKit, add explicit H before embedding, then generate
N conformers with a fixed recorded ETKDG configuration and seed. Freeze the actual
RDKit version/algorithm parameters during qualification, not an ambient default.
Set SmilesParserParams.removeHs=False before parsing: the pinned default removes
H and would lose explicit source-H identities before AddHs. Record the original
atom-index/element inventory, then call AddHs with explicitOnly=False and
addCoords=False before embedding. Original atoms remain the leading index range;
record each newly added H's parent from its bond, without inventing source IDs or
assuming arbitrary atom properties survive. Existing explicit H must not be
duplicated. Mixed explicit/implicit H must preserve the source H and add only the
missing H; genuinely H-free molecular graphs must add none. Qualify these cases
through the actual producer, not a standalone RDKit demonstration. Preserve the
settings, mapping and generated geometry in the result provenance.
Pinned primary references, checked 2026-10-19, and rationale:
https://github.com/rdkit/rdkit/blob/Release_2026_09_1/Code/GraphMol/SmilesParse/SmilesParse.h#L86-L94
  removeHs defaults true; preserving explicit source H requires false.
https://rdkit.org/docs/source/rdkit.Chem.rdmolops.html#rdkit.Chem.rdmolops.AddHs
  AddHs creates a new molecule; explicitOnly/addCoords govern H addition/coordinates.
https://github.com/rdkit/rdkit/blob/Release_2026_09_1/Code/GraphMol/AddHs.cpp#L526-L551
https://github.com/rdkit/rdkit/blob/Release_2026_09_1/Code/GraphMol/AddHs.cpp#L562-L596
  Original atom count is frozen before iteration; new H append and bond to their
  original parent. Mapping must be explicitly retained and tested by the producer.
No additional UFF/MMFF post-embedding optimization or energy/population weighting
in this slice; ETKDG itself includes distance-geometry optimization. Record
generated atom order and which H were added. Unspecified stereochemistry is not
resolved as an asserted experimental fact: disclose RDKit's generated choice and
preserve input stereochemical specification. N requests successful conformers:
if fewer are generated, return a concrete shortfall to the interpreter; never
silently reduce N or repeatedly reseed until success. A changed N requires source
authorization. Bound embedding attempts and CPU time; requested count is not a
guarantee of distinct conformers, and the guide must say so.

Plain text: accept an element/X/Y/Z table or one XYZ block with explicit units or
an explicitly documented input convention. Interpreter supplies source_text,
format and unit; deterministic code bounds and parses received text. Provenance
must say interpreter-supplied, not exact extraction from the Job. Require exactly
one geometry. Ambiguous columns,
unknown units or multiple geometries return actionable correction/request guidance.
The existing declared coordinate-JSON route remains a one-geometry shortcut with
the same admission; malformed declared JSON cannot fall through to interpretation.

Uploads: authenticated source references select exact immutable bytes, not host
paths. Instructions may select records or all records. Ambiguous selection requests
guidance rather than silently taking the first. Preserve original record/frame IDs,
including gaps after explicit selection. No molecular-equivalence check. Parse
every selected record before inference; a failed selected record fails preparation
with its identity and reason, never silently disappears from a successful batch.
Source access rejects Upload IDs outside this Job's authenticated grants regardless
of interpreter arguments. Bind inspected/selected record identities to the admitted
Upload digest; later fetching must not silently substitute changed source bytes.

Target acceptance catalogue (each row needs contained fixtures before advertising):
The offering advertises only the qualified allowlist and supported variants below,
never arbitrary structure formats by extension. All listed rows are 003 targets.
- XYZ: one block or a concatenated frame stream. Own strict frame splitting/counts;
  RDKit's single-block reader alone does not establish multi-frame support.
- MOL and SDF: single MOL or SDF record stream, V2000/V3000 variants qualified
  separately. Preserve supplied H and atom order; reject missing usable coordinates.
- PDB: explicit MODEL selection or all models; preserve serial/record mapping.
  Never silently merge alternate locations. Require an explicit supported alternate-
  location choice when present; reject unsupported ambiguity with precise guidance.
  Qualify element extraction and missing-H behavior rather than guessing from names.
- MOL2: documented concrete SYBYL atom types, two explicit sulfur aliases and the
  declared bare-element extension; coordinate-only projection, no custom type sets
  or graph/charge/attribute interpretation. Preserve the limitations in the guide.
  Split multiple molecule records explicitly and qualify each selected record.
Do not promise generic archives, trajectories or every dialect by file extension.
Unrecognized formats report supported alternatives. Do not add an Open Babel or
other fallback dependency without a concrete unsupported-format requirement.

For uploaded/pasted geometries preserve supplied atoms and coordinates: disable
implicit H removal, reordering and coordinate generation. A missing-H or 2D drawing
does not authorize inventing geometry. Explain which H predictions cannot be made;
if generation is wanted, require the explicit SMILES generation route. Planarity
alone is not evidence of a 2D drawing. Reader sanitization/bond perception must not
silently change the scientific input; qualify actual reader settings and mapping.
Supplied geometry is predicted as supplied; neither its coordinates nor predicted
shifts independently verify stereochemical identity/configuration. State this in
the guide and result limits, alongside generated-geometry uncertainty.

Units and plausibility
-----------------------
Normalize using a small declared unit vocabulary (angstrom, nm, bohr), with aliases
handled explicitly. Source declarations or documented format conventions establish
units; contradictory evidence returns feedback. Record original unit, evidence,
conversion constant/reference and factor. Obtain precise unit references before
implementing constants. Apply conversion once from original coordinates; validate
finite float32 values after conversion. Never rescale an already converted candidate.

Bond-length plausibility is diagnostic, not proof of units or correct chemistry.
Use supplied bonds where available, marking any inferred bonds separately; no graph
is required for coordinate-only input. Report implicated record/atom pair, observed
distance and rule/reference. Do not silently optimize, change atoms or infer a unit
from the best-fitting length. Qualify a small documented set of diagnostic rules;
unknown bond types remain unchecked, not invalid. Existing CH3Cl/CH2Cl2 NIST tests
are regression evidence, not universal bond-length acceptance thresholds. Warnings
accompany admitted results unless an independently justified structural rule fails.

Conversation and effects
------------------------
Expose a small set of typed operations: inspect a source; prepare supplied geometry
with format/selection/units; generate from SMILES + N; report a source problem.
Conversion is a preparation option, not an arbitrary scale-factor tool. Successful
preparation terminally selects the internally retained candidate in that model
turn. No model-facing submit, opaque token echo or stale-reference state exists.
The actual PreparedAnalysis handle remains internal to the Attempt lifecycle.

State flow: authenticated source -> inspect/select -> deterministic preparation ->
admission -> retained execution identity -> sequential inference -> terminal result.
An expected parse/admission rejection returns structured location/cause/correction
feedback to the same conversation. The model may change source selection or options
only within the commissioner's instructions. Repeated failures consume the same
turn/time/work budget. No retry resets the budget or silently drops records.
Do not rerun successful embedding merely to confirm input selection.

Budget exhaustion reports the last useful failure and what remains unresolved.
Missing source data cannot be repaired by inventing it. Endpoint, storage, parser
crash, resource exhaustion and inference failures retain operational causes; only
expected input failures invite conversational correction. Errors, not secrets,
reach the appropriate API/GUI/operator surfaces. Do not claim model-authored source
problems are independently verified. Protect against instructions embedded in files.

Bound source bytes, selected and discovered records, atoms, generated conformers,
embedding attempts, parser work, model turns, prepared output and total wall time.
Publish concrete limits from measured fixtures before offering admission. Reject
over-limit work before expensive effects where possible; streaming consumption must
also enforce limits. Preparation is a contained worker with no external egress and
no engine socket. Host orchestration must terminate and confirm retirement of stuck
reader/embedding work, as for inference; cancelling a Python await is insufficient.
One active Attempt owns preparation and inference; no implicit parallel worker pool.
Apply the inference privilege profile to preparation: nonroot, read-only root and
exact input mounts, cap-drop ALL/no-new-privileges, no credentials/network/engine
socket, bounded private scratch and explicit measured CPU/memory/PID/no-swap limits.
Its host-owned retirement seam exists before runtime reader integration.
Qualify the installed worker's actual RDKit/native dependency closure and import
origins with no ambient workspace/system fallback. A changed reference version
blocks qualification until its version-matched fixtures are deliberately reviewed.

Batch results, reproducibility and recovery
------------------------------------------
Admit the complete selected batch before loading checkpoints/scalers; dictionary
admission may read the verified dictionary. Execute geometries
sequentially through unchanged predict_request; no ensemble or conformer averaging.
Publication is all-or-nothing across the batch. A later geometry failure yields an
honest failure, not a truncated success; protected diagnostics can retain earlier
progress, solely as diagnostics, not published per-geometry successes. Recovery
reconciles one exact Attempt and terminal result. Estimate result-size bounds before dispatch and qualify the final envelope
against API and GUI. Each result identifies its geometry and original atom mapping.

Retain actual generated/prepared coordinates, producer/settings/seed, conversion
and selection evidence in the scientific result envelope, published through the
existing core terminal-result owner. Access follows that Job's result authorization;
these values are not hello text, public logs or raw interpreter transcripts. Bound
the whole envelope before inference and verify actual API/GUI limits in slice 1.
If it cannot fit, revise the supported batch limit before enabling the offering;
do not invent a new artifact service or silently omit reproduction evidence.
A seed alone is not sufficient reproduction evidence. Bind all executed request
digests and collection order before dispatch through the existing Attempt owner.
Freeze admitted prepared bytes for the Attempt. Do not regenerate after uncertain
execution or rerun earlier conformers to recover a partially executed batch. Exact
terminal publication replay remains the existing owner's job. State retention and
authorization must cover scientific sources without exposing them in public logs.
Prepared bytes are Attempt-local until a terminal result is prepared. Exact terminal
bytes then use the core's existing durable replay retention. Before dispatch, retain
digests and producer/selection identities as already required; there is no promise
to resume partially executed science after a crash, so no separate durable input
cache is required. Successful result retention follows the API's existing result
lifetime; private temporary sources are retired with owned Attempt work. Failed
Attempts retain safe causal evidence, not an unbounded archive of source coordinates.

Focused qualification matrix
-----------------------------
For each route: source -> exact prepared atoms/coordinates -> admission -> per-atom
result correspondence. Use licensed/self-authored small fixtures with pinned hashes
and expected numerical observations; do not regenerate expectations from new readers.
For each file variant: single/multiple records, selected/all, preserved H/order,
malformed/truncated later record, units, unsupported syntax and actual read bounds.
PDB additionally exercises model/alternate-location choice; MOL2 unsupported atom
types; SDF V2000/V3000; XYZ trailing/missing rows and multiple-frame boundaries.
SMILES covers explicit/implicit H, specified/unspecified stereochemistry, invalid
SMILES, N bounds, shortfall, seed/config recording and worker timeout/retirement.
Conversion covers identity/nm/bohr, contradictory evidence, no cumulative scaling,
overflow and diagnostic-only bond warnings. Compare distances with independent
expected values, including the existing two NIST cases where applicable.
Loop cases cover correctable reader feedback, unsupported source, prompt injection,
budget exhaustion, failed prepared-reference lookup, operational failure and no
duplicate generation on submission. No raw source/secret leakage in error surfaces.
Retain 002 scientific regression witnesses separately; new geometry qualification
does not establish NMR accuracy. Scripted interpreter tests prove control flow;
an explicitly invoked real-model lane must prove actual selection/conversion behavior.

Interpreter, failure and recovery policy
========================================

Use the existing core interpreter seam if compatible, with a NMRNet capability.
Do not borrow G01 solvent or SECS spectrum semantics. Configure ordered endpoints,
bounded requests/repairs and an overall deadline. Fix and qualify budgets rather
than copying unrelated timings/counts. Bound actual response consumption and
trickle duration; complete interrupted I/O before connection reuse (014 W03/W08).

Repairs may fix format/source selection, not invent scientific content. Operational
endpoint/protocol failure may permit bounded fallback; a terminal model-reported
source problem must not be overruled by another endpoint. Attribute such a report
to its producer, distinct from verified input rejection, outage and inference failure.
Explain what request material reaches the configured interpreter (014 W04).

Before inference, repeat interpretation only under the declared Attempt policy;
retain the executed-coordinate digest and preparation/interpreter identity through
the selected Attempt recovery owner before dispatch. This identifies the submitted
candidate even if no terminal result becomes available; inability to retain this
identity blocks dispatch. Establish that seam in slice 1, not a second journal.
Once inference may have begun,
do not reinterpret/recompute to recover an unobserved outcome. Replay exact prepared
terminal bytes on publication retry. Persist intermediate input only for a concrete
recovery promise, using the existing owner, not a new sidecar journal (014 W02).

The core owns start/complete/fail uncertainty and exact Attempt reconciliation.
Never infer commit from transport success or timeout. Storage failure blocks unsafe
advancement. Expiry is server-owned and not renewed by progress. On confirmed expiry,
stop owned work, preserve evidence, and neither resend nor manufacture a replacement
Attempt. If completion committed before response loss, recover that success.
The GUI shows Expired without per-expiry recovery advice: offering/guide text must
explain this Attempt has no published result and direct users to resolve outstanding
work with the operator before deliberately creating another Job. Preserve other
Attempts' results; promise no automatic resubmission.

Errors, but not secrets, must maximally inform the actual recipient. Developers
need phase/cause/source evidence; operators need Attempt/service effect and recovery
actions in bounded protected diagnostics and inspection. Commissioners need safe
specific rejection/unavailable/unresolved/failure reasons through API/GUI. Receivers
need results with limits or honest status. Stderr alone is not an acceptance surface.
Freeze each surface's owner, authorization, safe fields, bounds and retention.

Preserve primary/secondary causes, effect state and visible truncation. Do not map
all ValueError to input error. Bounding an oversized reason must preserve ordinary
rejection through API/GUI. Exercise model reports, tool history, nested exceptions,
captured output and storage/cleanup failures with private sentinels. Retain the
known scaler-version warning in protected diagnostics and disclose its compatibility
limit appropriately; no silent dependency refresh or tolerance relaxation.

Stopped inspection is one recovery step, not resolution. Use authenticated API
reconciliation and the existing owner's closed-record archive operation, bound to
the exact Attempt/record and reason. Preserve original evidence; closure does not
prove delivery. Unavailable API/in-progress Attempt remains held. Document restart
after archival and confirmed worker retirement; never delete journals to clear a hold.

Contained deployment and normal service
=======================================

All Python, package work, fixtures, tests and science run in containers. Host
shell/Make/Git orchestrate. Acquisition follows existing explicit-interface,
advisory-review and lock workflows; builds consume reviewed offline inputs. Lock
refresh reviews the whole package-set diff, not index freshness. No upstream or
sibling changes without prior discussion.
Deployment orchestration remains host shell. Python configuration, credential and
state validation runs in contained helpers with only its required mounts; the host
shell invokes admitted engine operations. SECS/NMRPeak host-Python deployment
controllers are workflow precedents, not an exception to this containment rule.

Named workflow: initialize private config/state; install API-issued signing artifact
and separate interpreter credentials; build admitted images; preview configuration;
start; status/logs; stop/restart. API authority owns registration/issuance. Provider
installation must not obtain API runtime secrets or silently replace identity.
Use disposable local qualification credentials (014 W06/W07).

Bind retained state to API origin/provider identity and exclude competing owners.
Keep admitted configuration stable for a run; record config/image identity and make
rotation/edits/redeployment explicit. Reuse suitable snapshot/locking mechanisms,
not every sibling confirmation scheme. Down removes only owned runtime resources;
configuration, artifacts and retained work survive. No blanket clean operation.

Provider grants cover API/interpreter, and source access only if required. Admit
per-destination TLS trust: normal roots or explicit private CA, never verification
bypass. External access requires an explicit host interface, enforced sole route
and direct-bypass-denial proof; the existing host-network acquisition proxy alone
is insufficient. Local API/GUI uses a named internal network with tested external-
egress denial. Live model calls use a separate explicit external grant.
For API, interpreter, runner and any admitted source exchanges, prove whole-operation
deadlines and byte/work bounds, including trickling or failed responses, plus
completed interruption and transport release before reuse. Per-read timeout alone
is insufficient; reuse the actual transport owner's mechanism (013 X04), including
M:magnet_provider/provider_http.py and runner_session.py as behavioral precedents.
Slice 1 must prove the binding uses the local provider-core owners from 017 and
identify missing behavior there; it must not import the Magnet package.

Inference stays offline/read-only/nonroot, cap-drop ALL/no-new-privileges, 2 CPUs,
4 GiB/no swap, 64 PIDs and bounded scratch. Provider CPU/memory/PID/scratch/storage
also get measured bounds. Mount only needed sources/socket/state; no service engine
socket, home or whole workspace. Bound actual reads, including partial/failing
ones and source growth; preserve existing same-buffer artifact consumption.

One active inference; reject concurrent admission. Host orchestration owns startup
and retirement. Cancellation or uncertain in-flight execution retires the exact
runner container; confirm engine termination before replacement/new inference.
If confirmation fails, admission stays blocked. Name and prove the bounded host
control route early; helper death/socket loss alone is insufficient. Successful
requests and determinate rejections may reuse the runner.
Cleanup of shared names/markers/handles requires matching owned identity; preserve
replacement objects. Private exclusive scratch needs no extra identity ceremony.

Normal operation publishes complete hello periodically, discovers eligible work,
reports progress and recovers temporary outages under bounded policy. Configuration
rejection stays visible. Container health, accepted hello, API availability,
interpreter reachability and scientific readiness are different facts. Define
truthful readiness for sequential model loading, without forcing resident H/C heads.
Keep offering descriptions within 1,024 Unicode scalars: concise input/limits and
full copyable guide URL; complete rights-cleared examples live in the guide.
Test the delivered text, not just its template (014 W09/W12).

Review discipline
=================

Coordinator authors/integrates; Luna-low agents challenge the end-user workflow,
science/U/A, operations/containment and API/error delivery. After material corrections,
require two complete unchanged-text rounds without actionable findings and record
concise dispositions/identity, not raw logs. During implementation audit every small
meaningful slice against important U/A and ordinary code concerns, with focused
contained tests and commits using explicit -- FILE paths. Plan review is not runtime
proof; do not manufacture requirements merely to prolong it.

R33. Planned configurable significant digits with exact private evidence
-----------------------------------------------------------------------
The user requested fewer output digits and a configurable setting. The chosen
default for [output] shift_significant_digits is 4, strictly a built-in
integer from 1 through 17; booleans are invalid. Project only admitted H/C shift_ppm
values to numeric JSON after scientific-result admission. Use stdlib float format
with general precision and nearest-even rounding of the actual binary64 value,
then a numeric value, not fixed-width display strings. For example,
13.897350311279297 becomes 13.9 at 4 digits; JSON numbers do not retain trailing
significance zeros. Rounding is output policy, not experimental accuracy evidence.
Primary Python3.12 documentation verified for this choice:
https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/string.html#format-specification-mini-language
defines g precision as significant digits, removes insignificant trailing zeros
and states that float formatting uses the round() builtin's rounding mode.
https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/functions.html#round specifies nearest/even
ties and explains why binary float representation can differ from decimal intent.
Thus float(format(value, f'.{digits}g')) deliberately follows the admitted binary64
value, without decimal half-up reinterpretation or a display-width promise.

Inspected precedent: magnet-deploy 600968a5fad8ad0118ea4f42cd7fc1fbfde54aaf,
models/e_01_jvae_nmrexp/magnet/stairway/e_01_jvae_nmrexp/client_input/__init__.py::
_round_significant (lines 125–144), called by as_json_dict at fixed 6 digits
(lines 231–239). That stdlib Decimal(str(value))/ROUND_HALF_UP helper rounds INPUT
serialization; it is not a configurable prediction-output policy. NMRNet's chosen
binary64 nearest-even contract deliberately differs and is not a copied helper.
No sibling output default is claimed. No sigfig dependency is needed.

Freeze the chosen policy in the existing ResultPlan envelope/prepared identity
before execution. Prepared coordinates, request/source hashes, native model
arithmetic, selected artifacts and runtime facts remain exact. Preserve unrounded
admitted FLOAT prediction values in existing ProtectedEvidence, not original wire
or integer encoding; normalizing an admitted exact integer to its equal float
preserves the existing numeric contract.
Use the same plan's 32-character finite-binary64 placeholders to reserve the known
terminal-evidence overhead, combined with actual known preparation/interpreter
diagnostics. Session preparation must pass existing ProtectedEvidence admission
before prediction dispatch. This reserves known overhead, not unknown future
native warning text. Existing dynamic overflow still retains the whole original
worker outcome and prepared identity through ResultAssemblyFailed; no evidence
limit, journal or lifecycle framework is added and science is not rerun.

Scientific qualification staging explicitly selects 17 digits to preserve existing
exact numerical oracles; ordinary configuration defaults to 4. Tests first cover
4/17 digits, invalid/bool settings, ties, carry, zero, tiny values, finite overflow,
raw-result admission, every geometry, exact private evidence, pre-dispatch capacity
and replay of frozen bytes. No coordinate/shift expected output is regenerated,
no tolerance is changed, and no native/model/API call is planned for this slice.
This records the design only; precision behavior is not yet implemented or
qualified, and full003 remains open.

R34. Interpreter-selected precision supersedes the R33 configuration plan
------------------------------------------------------------------------
The user clarified that precision should be an interpreter input/output with a
system-defined default. This supersedes R33's planned TOML [output] setting and
staging override; the dated rounding/evidence rationale above remains applicable.
No TOML, runtime or independent Session precision knob will be introduced.

Expose the code-owned default of 4 significant digits and the strict built-in
integer range 1 through 17 in the interpreter prompt and all three terminal
prepare-tool schemas. shift_significant_digits is optional: omission uses 4;
null, booleans, fractional values and out-of-range integers are correctable input
rejections before source acquisition or native allocation. Required molecular
fields remain required and unknown fields remain rejected. The existing prepare
call selects both molecular input and precision; no extra model exchange or
post-preparation mutation is needed.

on each immutable supplied/generated candidate. That candidate is the sole
selection authority. ResultPlan reads its value directly, freezes it in the
existing prepared identity/result output field, and applies R33's admitted-value
rounding. Session carries no separate choice. Existing protected evidence will retain
unrounded admitted floats, with the same plan's known-overhead reservation and
pre-dispatch admission; unknown warning growth keeps the existing failure path.

The four actual-API qualification Job fixtures will explicitly request 17 digits;
scripted preparation arguments also select 17 through the unchanged model peer.
Direct/native qualification choosers select 17 where an existing numerical oracle
requires it. No qualification-only staging override or new expected output is
introduced. Tests must cover omitted/default and explicit selection across all
routes, correction before effects, frozen identity/raw evidence and existing exact
oracles. This is the revised plan, not completed implementation or a new live
qualification. No native/model/API call is authorized by this entry; 003 stays open.

singleton and retirement owners remain responsible. No new daemon, journal,
configuration grammar, automatic recovery sweep or scientific authority is added.

Inspection reads the same closed TOML envelope and lifecycle/scope declarations,
then opens the existing store under its lock. It requires no signing credential,
CA or model-key read and constructs no API transport. It reports owned metadata,
including existing TerminalHeld problem_code/request_id and SuppressedOutcome
reason, without terminal bodies or private evidence. Its record_sha256 is SHA-256
of existing encode_record(record), the admitted current snapshot, not arbitrary
original file spelling. Run selects exactly one uniquely current SHA under that
same store lock, before API construction, then uses the existing core claims and
recovery operation once. Missing/stale/nonunique selection sends nothing.

Run admits only the existing API credential/trust and lifecycle policies. Capture
first reads TOML-only metadata, then grants only its selected individual files;
original bytes and absolute path layout survive without TOML rewriting. The
stable state mount remains separate. No model credential, molecular input or
scientific worker is granted to the recovery process. TerminalPending reuses the
exact body/evidence; confirmed records send nothing. A replayed StartPending may
become acknowledged but stays locally unresolved; entered execution is observed,
never resumed. Unknown entered states, held conflicts and suppressed outcomes
remain held. No archival/deletion or synthesized terminal failure is introduced.
local_obligations_settled does not mean ready for fresh work: ordinary startup
still checks storage capacity and current provider-wide API inventory. Pending
I/O, interruption effect facts and independent cleanup failures retain their
existing owners; a held process does not release its store on the first signal.

R47. Bounded GUI ZIP consumption in the isolated reader
------------------------------------------------------
2026-10-23. First production slice adds Upload inspection and exact named-member
reading to the existing one-use GeometryClient/geometry_worker channel. Original

its existing max_worker_calls,
including failures and corrections. Therefore total visible expanded bytes across

Rationale and proof scope
-------------------------
The seven cases address concrete gaps, not repeat the scientific matrix:
- Earlier successful cases supply the needed units/count/selection. They cannot
  establish whether the interpreter guesses missing values or notices that no
  supported molecular input was supplied. These four explicit omissions require
  a safe owned input-problem outcome before preparation. Problem categories are
  existing user-actionable tool choices; the check does not demand exact wording
  or a particular free-form explanation.
- Earlier XYZ/SDF/SMILES decisions and deterministic reader tests cannot establish
  real-model selection of MOL/PDB/MOL2 options. These formats have different
  record/model/alternate-site conventions; wrong format or [1,2] instead of the
  explicitly requested[2,1] can fail or change the scientific input. The three
  cases test those reader-specific arguments using real option admission.
  The single MOL uses the existing null-selection tool contract. They do not
  prescribe arbitrary explanatory wording, model inspection counts or a PDB
  alternate-site label that cannot affect this fixture.
The model was not told expected_issue, expected argument dictionaries or outputs.
No corrective preparation feedback was supplied. Successful format choice ends
selection without another model submit/token exchange. Counts above describe
these observations, not a prescribed dialogue length.

Job/Upload refs: authenticated_api_jobs=false, scientific_work_performed=false.

transfer, file-byte acquisition/parsing, RDKit generation or NMRNet prediction
ran. The four ambiguity decisions use6real exchanges total; the three format
choices use7. Total13paid exchanges,7cases, no new API Job or scientific rerun.
These qualify this selected endpoint/configuration on these authored instructions,
not arbitrary phrasing, universal language understanding, truthful chemistry in
